[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/18:33:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/18:33:3)


**לִמְקוֹמוֹ.** אֶל בֵּיתוֹ.


למקומו, to his home.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ שְׁנֵי הַמַּלְאָכִים סְדֹמָה בָּעֶרֶב.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִגִּיעוּ שָׁם בְּלִי אִחוּר, כְּאָמְרָם (ברכות ד:) גַּבְרִיאֵל בִּשְׁתַּיִם מִיכָאֵל בְּאַחַת, וְכֵן הוֹרָה לְמַעְלָה בְּאָמְרוֹ "וַיֵּלְכוּ סְדֹמָה" (בראשית יח:כב), מִכָּל מָקוֹם לֹא נִכְנְסוּ בִּסְדוֹם עַד הָעֶרֶב, שֶׁכְּבָר נִסְתַּתְּמוּ טַעֲנוֹת אַבְרָהָם הַמֵּלִיץ וְנִגְמַר הַדִּין.


ויבאו שני המלאכים סדומה בערב, even though they arrived at that city without “time” having elapsed since they departed from Avraham, (compare Berachot 4 the angel Gavriel needing two steps, and the angel Michael only one step) they remained outside the city gates, invisible, not entering and appearing to Lot until evening. The same is true of 18,22 וילכו סדומה. Avraham had concluded his dialogue with G’d already some time previously.


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:2)


**וַיָּקָם לִקְרָאתָם.** פֶּן יָלִינוּ בָּרְחוֹב, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה אָז הַמִּנְהָג בִּשְׁאָר הֶעָרִים כְּשֶׁאֵין מַכְנִיס אוֹרְחִים, וְיַזִּיקוּ לָהֶם רִשְׁעֵי הָעִיר:


ויקם לקראתם, in order to prevent them from spending the night in the street. This was customary also in cities other than Sodom when no one had offered shelter to unexpected strangers. In such instances there was always the danger that the strangers would be harmed by some of the residents of the city.


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:1:3)


**וַיִּשְׁתַּחוּ אַפַּיִם אָרְצָה.** כִּי הָיָה תָּאֳרָם נוֹרָא בְּלִי סָפֵק.


וישתחו אפים ארצה, they clearly looked sufficiently imposing to warrant being treated deferentially.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:3:1)


**מִשְׁתֶּה.** קְבִיעוּת סְעוּדָה עַל הַיַּיִן, בִּהְיוֹת הַיַּיִן אָהוּב אֶצְלוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹכִיחַ סוֹפוֹ עַל תְּחִלָּתוֹ (בראשית יט:לג). אֲבָל אַבְרָהָם לֹא עָשָׂה מִשְׁתֶּה, זוּלָתִי "בְּיוֹם הִגָּמֵל אֶת יִצְחָק" (בראשית כא:ח) לִגְדוֹלֵי הַדּוֹר שֶׁהָיוּ מֻרְגָּלִים בְּכָךְ בְּעֵת שִׂמְחָתָם, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (סוטה י): "קַמֵּי דְּשָׁתֵי חַמְרָא, חַמְרָא".


**A feast.** Literally, “a drinking feast.” Lot offered them wine because he was fond of it himself.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:8:1)


**אוֹצִיאָה נָּא אֶתְהֶן אֲלֵיכֶם.** חָשַׁב שֶׁיָּקוּמוּ לוֹקְחֵי בְנוֹתָיו, וְקָאם שָׁאוֹן בֵּינֵיהֶם (הושע י:יד).


אוציאה נא אתהן אליכן, he hoped that the fiancés of his daughters would come to their aid. (compare Hoseah 10,14 for the meaning of the author’s expression קאם שאון).



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:9:1)


**גֶּשׁ הָלְאָה.** מֵעַל הַפֶּתַח וְנִשְׁבֹּר **הַדֶּלֶת**:


גש הלאה, “away from the door so that we can break it down;”


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:9:2)


**הָאֶחָד בָּא לָגוּר.** הֲיֵשׁ גַּם אֶחָד שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה כָּךְ?


האחד בא לגור, “is there even a single man who can be audacious enough to get away with this?”



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:10:1)


**וְאֶת הַדֶּלֶת סָגָרוּ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁ**יִּלְאוּ לִמְצֹא הַפָּתַח**, וִיפַרְסְמוּ גֹּדֶל רִשְׁעָם:


**They wearied themselves.** They continued searching for the door even after they were stricken with blindness.



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:11:1)


**וַיִּלְאוּ לִמְצֹא הַפָּתַח.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהָיוּ מֻכִּים **בַּסַּנְוֵרִים**, טָרְחוּ לִמְצֹא הַפֶּתַח כְּדֵי **לִשְׁבֹּר הַדֶּלֶת**, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (עירובין יט.): "רְשָׁעִים אֲפִלּוּ עוֹמְדִים בְּפִתְחוֹ שֶׁל גֵּיהִנֹּם אֵינָם חוֹזְרִים בִּתְשׁוּבָה".


וילאו למצוא הפתח, even though they had already been smitten with blindness they did not therefore desist from their wicked intention. This is in line with the psychological insight of our sages in Eyruvin 19 that the wicked will not become penitents even when they stand at the gates of hell. [the Nazis persisting in the “final solution,” even while already defeated by the Russsians are the best example of this insight we know of. Ed.]



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:14:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא לוֹט.** אַחַר שֶׁנִּלְאוּ וְהָלְכוּ לָהֶם:


ויצא לוט, after they had given up and they had dispersed.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:15:1)


**וַיָּאִיצוּ.** כְּדֵי שֶׁתְּהֵא פּוּרְעָנוּתָם בִּזְרִיחַת הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ אֱלֹהֵיהֶם הַגָּדוֹל, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (ברכות ז.) בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהַחַמָּה זוֹרַחַת וְכָל מַלְכֵי מִזְרָח וּמַעֲרָב מַנִּיחִין כִּתְרֵיהֶן עַל רָאשֵׁיהֶם וּמִשְׁתַּחֲוִים לַחַמָּה, מִיָּד כּוֹעֵס.


ויאיצו, in order that their destruction should take place at the very moment the sun, their great god, would come forth. (compare Berachot 7)



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:16:1)


**בְּחֶמְלַת ה' עָלָיו.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבִּזְכוּת אַבְרָהָם הָיָה נִמְלָט, כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיִּזְכֹּר אֱלֹהִים אֶת אַבְרָהָם וַיִּשְׁלַח אֶת לוֹט מִתּוֹךְ הַהֲפֵכָה**, הִנֵּה כֵּיוָן שֶׁהָיָה מְאַחֵר וּמִתְעַכֵּב אַחַר אַזְהָרַת הַמַּלְאָכִים, הָיָה רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת נִסְפֶּה, אֲבָל הָיְתָה הַחֶמְלָה עָלָיו, כִּי לֹא בְּמֶרֶד וְלֹא בְּמַעַל הָיָה עִכּוּבוֹ, אֶלָּא מִצַּד עַצְלָה וְנֶפֶשׁ נִבְהָלָה:


**For Ad-noy had pity.** Even though Lot was originally spared in Avraham’s merit, he would have been killed for tarrying if Hashem had not had pity on him.



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:17:1)


**אַל תַּבִּיט אַחֲרֶיךָ.** כִּי הָרָעָה תִּתְפַּשֵּׁט עָדֶיךָ כִּמְהַלֶּכֶת אַחֲרֶיךָ וְלֹא תַּזִּיקְךָ, אֲבָל אִם תִּתְעַכֵּב לְהַבִּיט תִּדְבַּק בְּךָ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁקָּרָה לְאִשְׁתּוֹ כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַתְּהִי נְצִיב מֶלַח**.


אל תביט אחריך, the evil would catch up with you as soon as you interrupt your march away from it. This is precisely what happened to his wife when she ignored the instructions and looked behind. (verse 26)



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:24:1)


**מֵאֵת ה' מִן הַשָּׁמָיִם.** לֹא שֶׁהֶעֱלָה נְשִׂיאִים מִקְצֵה הָאָרֶץ, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁיִּתְהַלְּכוּ בָּאֲוִיר כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּקְרֶה בַּבְּרָקִים וְאַבְנֵי אֵשׁ בַּטֶּבַע.


מאת ה' מן השמים, this was not a natural event such as a volcanic eruption, the ash, brimstone and lava afterwards descending on the surrounding earth. The source of this destruction did not originate in the bowels of the earth.



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:25:1)


**וַיַּהֲפֹךְ.** שֶׁנֶּהְפַּךְ הָאָרֶץ הַהִיא וְיוֹשְׁבֶיהָ לְטֶבַע גָּפְרִית וָאֵשׁ, כְּמוֹ ״הָפַךְ אֶת מֵימֵיהֶם לְדָם״ (תהלים קה:כט), וּבָהֶם נֶהְפַּךְ לְטֶבַע מֶלַח אוֹתוֹ הַטַּל שֶׁעָלָה קוֹדֶם הַזְּרִיחָה, כְּאָמְרוֹ ״גָּפְרִית וָמֶלַח שְׂרֵפָה כָל אַרְצָהּ״ (דברים כט:כב), כְּמִשְׁפַּט כָּל לַחוּת תָּפֵל מְעוֹרָב עִם חֲלָקִים שְׂרוּפִים:


ויהפוך, the earth as well as its inhabitants underwent a total chemical transformation being turned into brimstone and fire, similar to what David means in Psalms 105,29 “He turned their waters into blood.” The dew that rose from the earth prior to sunrise turned into salt. Compare Deut. 29,22 גפרית ומלח שרפה כל ארצה, meaning that the moisture descended back onto earth but this time mixed with solid burned particles.



### Verse 27

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:27:1)


**אֶל הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר עָמַד שָׁם אֶת פְּנֵי ה'.** אֶל הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁלִּוָּה שָׁם הַמַּלְאָכִים, כִּי שָׁם הָיְתָה עָלָיו יַד ה', וְזֶה כִּי חָשַׁב לְבַקֵּשׁ עֲלֵיהֶם רַחֲמִים אַחֲרֵי שֶׁלֹּא מָצָא לָהֶם זְכוּת בַּדִּין.


אל המקום אשר עמד שם את פני ה, to the place to which he had accompanied the angels. This is where the punishing hand of G’d had first appeared to him. Since he had not been able to save the inhabitants of Sodom legally, he prepared to intercede for them by asking G’d s mercy for them.



### Verse 28

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:28:1)


**וַיַּשְׁקֵף.** הַשְׁקָפַת אֵיבָה עַל רוֹב רִשְׁעָם.


וישקף, a hostile kind of looking, motivated by their excess wickedness.


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:28:2)


**וַיַּרְא וְהִנֵּה עָלָה קִיטוֹר הָאָרֶץ.** וּבְכֵן רָאָה שֶׁאֵין עוֹד לְהִתְפַּלֵּל עֲלֵיהֶם.


קיטור הארץ, when he saw the smoke he realised that it was too late to pray on behalf of these people.



### Verse 29

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:29:1)


**מִתּוֹךְ הַהֲפֵכָה בַּהֲפוֹךְ אֶת הֶעָרִים.** בִּהְיוֹת שֶׁנִּמְלַט בִּזְכוּת אַבְרָהָם מִתּוֹךְ הַהֲפֵכָה, בְּעוֹד שֶׁהָיָה הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ הוֹפֵךְ אֶת הֶעָרִים קוֹדֶם שֶׁיָּצָא לוֹט מִכָּל הַכִּכָּר, וְלָזֶה לֹא הָיָה זוֹכֶה בְּשׁוּם פָּנִים אַחֲרֵי שֶׁלֹּא נִמְלַט קוֹדֶם הַתְחָלַת הַפּוּרְעָנוּת בְּעַצְלוּתוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיִּתְמַהְמָהּ**. לְפִיכָךְ יָרֵא לָשֶׁבֶת בְּצוֹעַר, כִּי חָשַׁב שֶׁהַהֲפֵכָה תַּמְתִּין גַּם לְצוֹעַר עַד שֶׁיִּמָּלֵט הוּא מִמֶּנָּה בִּזְכוּת אַבְרָהָם, וְשֶׁאַחֲרֵי צֵאתוֹ מִשָּׁם תִּתְפַּשֵּׁט הַהֲפֵכָה גַּם בְּצוֹעַר:


מתוך ההפכה בהפוך את הערים, seeing that he had escaped by the merit of Avraham from the destruction of these cites, a process which had begun even before Lot had been outside the outer perimeters of the valley due to his laziness. (compare verse 16) As a result, he was afraid to settle in Tzoar thinking that its destruction had been delayed only on account of his having taken refuge there.



### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:30:1)


**וַיֵּשֶׁב בָּהָר.** כִּי חָשַׁב שֶׁהַהֲפֵכָה תִּתְפַּשֵּׁט **בְּכָל הַכִּכָּר** עַד הָהָר, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹרָה הַמַּלְאָךְ בְּאָמְרוֹ **הָהָרָה הִמָּלֵט**.


וישב בהר, because he thought that the entire valley would be destroyed, excluding the surrounding mountains.



### Verse 31

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:31:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:31:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:31:1)


**אָבִינוּ זָקֵן.** וְלֹא יִתְאַמֵּץ לְהַרְחִיק נְדוֹד אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת:


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:31:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:31:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:31:2)


**וְאִישׁ אֵין בָּאָרֶץ לָבוֹא עָלֵינוּ.** אֵין אִישׁ בָּזֶה הַגָּלִיל רָאוּי לָבוֹא עָלֵינוּ כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל הָאָרֶץ, שֶׁהַמִּנְהָג הוּא שֶׁלֹּא תִּנָּשֵׂא הָאִשָּׁה אֶלָּא לֶהָגוּן לָהּ:



### Verse 37

###### Sforno on Genesis 19:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:37:1)


**וַתִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ מוֹאָב... וַתִּקְרָא שְׁמוֹ בֶּן עַמִּי.** לְהוֹרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא נִתְעַבְּרוּ מֵאָדָם בִּלְתִּי הָגוּן:


ותקרא שמו מואב...ותקרא שמו בן עמי. They called the names of their children thus to show the world that the father was not someone who was an unworthy, sinful person. [from the point of view of Jewish law as it pertains to gentiles, a father may sleep with his daughter. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Genesis 19:37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/19:37:2)


**הוּא אֲבִי מוֹאָב. הוּא אֲבִי בְנֵי עַמּוֹן.** שֶׁיָּרְשׁוּ אֶרֶץ, כִּי מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהָיְתָה כַּוָּנַת הַנָּשִׁים רְצוּיָה, הָיָה זַרְעָם לִשְׁנֵי גּוֹיִם יוֹרְשֵׁי אַבְרָהָם בִּקְצָת, כְּאָמְרָם (משלי ג:ו) "בְּכָל דְּרָכֶיךָ דָעֵהוּ" אֲפִילוּ לִדְבַר עֲבֵרָה (נזיר כג).


הוא אבי מואב...הוא אבי בני עמון, the ones who would in a short while inherit large parts of what would eventually be part of the land of Israel on the east bank of the Jordan river. Seeing that the intentions of both daughters in sleeping with their father had been honourable, they were rewarded with founding two nations, both from the same father. (Nazir 23) In a certain sense they had become heirs of Avraham to whom all the Canaanite lands had been promised. This reflects the statement in Proverbs 3,6 בכל דרכיך דעהו, “know (try to emulate) G’d in all your undertakings;” (even when involving an apparent sin. [based on Berachot 63. Ed.]



## Chapter 20


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:1:1)


**וַיֵּשֶׁב בֵּין קָדֵשׁ וּבֵין שׁוּר.** בֵּין שְׁתֵּי עֲיָרוֹת גְּדוֹלוֹת, כְּדֵי לִקְרֹא שָׁם בְּשֵׁם ה׳ אֵל עוֹלָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁעָשָׂה אַחַר כָּךְ לְהַגְבִּיר בְּרִית לָרַבִּים.


וישב בין קדש ובי שור, between 2 large cities, in order to have an opportunity to proclaim the holy name of G’d in both these cities, as he did later, the objective being to improve G’d’s image among mankind.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:3:1)


**וַיָּבֹא אֱלֹהִים אֶל אֲבִימֶלֶךְ.** כְּעִנְיָן שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּלָבָן (בראשית לא:כד) וּבְבִלְעָם (במדבר כב:כד), וְלֹא נִזְכַּר בָּהֶם אָז "וַיֵּרָא אֵלָיו" וְלֹא "מַרְאוֹת אֱלֹהִים" וְלֹא "דִּבּוּר", כְּמוֹ שֶׁנִּזְכַּר בָּאָבוֹת וּבִשְׁאָר הַנְּבִיאִים, כְּאָמְרוֹ "בַּמַּרְאָה אֵלָיו אֶתְוַדָּע בַּחֲלוֹם אֲדַבֶּר בּוֹ" (במדבר יב:ו), כִּי אָמְנָם לֹא נִרְאָה הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ כְּלָל אָז, אֲבָל בָּא אֲלֵיהֶם קוֹל אוֹמֵר בִּלְבַד.


ויבא אלוקים אל אבימלך, this visitation was of a similar kind to that reported with Lavan (31,24) and Bileam (Numbers 22,24) respectively. The Torah refrains from speaking about a מחזה, “a vision,” in order not to place these wicked people on a pedestal similar to our patriarchs. The expression דבור to describe a verbal communication to these individuals is also absent, G’d having reserved such an expression for communications with prophets, as He pointed out to Miriam and Aaron in Numbers 12,6. Avimelech did not experience a visual image of G’d in his dream at all. He perceived a divine voice addressing him.


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:3:2)


**הִנְּךָ מֵת.** הִנְּךָ הוֹלֵךְ וְכָלֶה בַּחוֹלִי שֶׁהִתְחִיל בְּךָ, **כִּי עָצֹר עָצַר ה׳**:


הנך מת, the impotence which you experienced (verse 18) will result in your death.



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:4:1)


**הֲגוֹי גַּם צַדִּיק תַּהֲרֹג.** הַאֻמְנָם רָאוּי שֶׁתַּשְׁחִית הַגּוֹי בַּהֲרִיגַת מַלְכּוֹ, וְשֶׁתַּהֲרֹג הַמֶּלֶךְ שֶׁהוּא צַדִּיק בָּזֶה שֶׁלֹּא חָטָא?


הגוי גם צדיק, is it fair that You would kill a nation by killing its king, especially when this king is not even guilty of a mortal offence?



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:1)


**וְעַתָּה הָשֵׁב אֶת אֵשֶׁת הָאִישׁ.** בְּטֶרֶם שֶׁתִּכְלֶה בַּחוֹלִי:


ועתה השב את אשת האיש, before you perish from the sickness.


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:2)


**וֶחְיֵה.** תֵּרָפֵא, כְּמוֹ "עַד חֱיוֹתָם" (יהושע ה:ח).


וחיה, and be healed and stay alive; we find a similar construction in Joshua 5,8 עד חיותם, “until they have been healed.”


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:7:3)


**אַתָּה וְכָל אֲשֶׁר לָךְ.** הַוְּלָדוֹת בִּמְעֵי אִשְׁתְּךָ וְאַמְהוֹתֶיךָ:


אתה וכל אשר לך, all the fetuses whose progress had been arrested within their mothers’ wombs.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:9:1)


**מַעֲשִׂים אֲשֶׁר לֹא יֵעָשׂוּ.** לִגְרֹם הֶזֵּק לְאִישׁ לֹא יְדַעְתּוֹ קֹדֶם לָכֵן, וְאֵין לְךָ אֵיבָה עִמּוֹ, וְכָל זֶה בִּלְתִּי תּוֹעַלְתְּךָ, וְאֵין דֶּרֶךְ לַעֲשׂוֹת כֵּן.


**Deeds that ought not to be done.** You brought harm to a stranger with whom you had no prior relationship of enmity, and without profit to yourself.



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:11:1)


**רַק אֵין יִרְאַת אֱלֹהִים בַּמָּקוֹם הַזֶּה.** אֵין מוֹרָא מַלְכוּת, כִּי אָמְנָם סַרְנֵי פְלִשְׁתִּים לֹא הָיוּ מְלָכִים גְּמוּרִים נוֹרָאִים בָּעָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוֹרָה גָּלְיָת בְּאָמְרוֹ "הֲלֹא אָנֹכִי הַפְּלִשְׁתִּי וְאַתֶּם עֲבָדִים לְשָׁאוּל" (שמואל א יז:ח).


רק אין יראת אלוקים במקום הזה, there is no respect for the government’s authority. The Philistines did not have kings, but their political leaders were known as סרנים, as we know from the story of Goliath, who was a law unto himself due to his personal strength and was not subject to the authority of such סרנים. Compare Goliath’s boast הלא אנכי הפלשתי ואתם עבדים לשאול, “am I not the Philistine, whereas you are slaves of Sha-ul.” (Samuel I 17,8)



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:12:1)


**וְגַם אָמְנָה.** וְגַם אַתָּה הַמֶּלֶךְ הַצַּדִּיק בָּהֶם חָטָאתָ בָּזֶה שֶׁלָּקַחְתָּ הָאִשָּׁה בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי שֶׁהִיא **אֲחֹתִי**, שֶׁהָיָה לְךָ לִשְׁאֹל אִם הִיא גַּם כֵּן אִשְׁתִּי, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִיא בֶּאֱמֶת אֲחוֹתִי וְאִשְׁתִּי:


וגם אמנה, also you, the king himself, have sinned in taking the woman because I said she is my sister for you should have asked me if she was also my wife. The fact is that she is both my sister and my wife.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:13:1)


**הִתְעוּ אֹתִי אֱלֹהִים.** בְּסִבַּת עֲבוֹדַת גִּלּוּלִים שֶׁמָּאַסְתִּי, הֻצְרַכְתִּי לָצֵאת מִבֵּית אָבִי אֶל אֲשֶׁר לֹא אֵדַע, לֹא לְמָקוֹם מְכֻוָּן מֵאִתִּי, וּבָזֶה יִקָּרֵא תּוֹעֶה.


התעו אותי אלוקים, on account of the various alien gods which I despise I have been forced to leave my father’s house and to travel to areas I did not know, not to places of my own choice. This is why he described himself as תועה, someone who wanders, does not have a fixed destination.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:1)


**הִנֵּה נָתַתִּי אֶלֶף כֶּסֶף לְאָחִיךְ.** לְמֹהַר כְּמִנְהַג לוֹקֵחַ בַּת אִישׁ אוֹ אֲחוֹתוֹ לְאִשָּׁה.


הנה נתתי אלף כסף לאחיך, as dowry, as was the custom for any groom marrying the daughter or sister of a man.


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:2)


**הִנֵּה הוּא לָךְ כְּסוּת עֵינַיִם.** מַלְבּוּשׁ שֶׁל כַּמָּה גְּוָנִים שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹבְשׁוֹת הַנָּשִׁים לְכָבוֹד, כִּי אָמְנָם הַמֹּהַר שֶׁנָּתַתִּי מוֹרֶה עַל כְּבוֹדֵךְ, שֶׁלֹּא לְקַחְתִּיךְ לְזוֹנָה וְלֹא לְפִילֶגֶשׁ, אֲבָל לְקַחְתִּיךְ לְאִשָּׁה, וְלֹא עֲזַבְתִּיךְ כָּל כָּךְ מְהֵרָה לוּלֵי ה' שֶׁהָיָה לָךְ וְהֻצְרַכְתִּי לְהַחֲזִירֵךְ.


הנה לך כסות עינים, clothing consisting of a variety of colours which women used to wear as a mark of distinction. The dowry he gave her brother was intended to show that he did not consider her a whore but someone reputable, and that he did not mean her to be his concubine but his regular wife. Also, he meant that he would not have let Sarah go so easily except for Divine intervention which forced him to let her go.


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:3)


**לְכֹל אֲשֶׁר אִתָּךְ.** לְעֵינֵי כָּל אֲשֶׁר אִתָּךְ וּבְנֵי בֵיתֵךְ:


לכל אשר אתך, before the eyes of everybody, so that they know in your household that everything is above board.


###### Sforno on Genesis 20:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:16:4)


**וְאֵת כֹּל וְנוֹכָחַת.** וּבְעֵינֵי כָּל שׁוֹמֵעַ וּבְעֵינֵי כֹּל נוֹכַחַת, שֶׁתְּבַקֵּשׁ אוּלַי לִגְנוּתֵךְ כְּמִנְהַג הַנָּשִׁים הַמּוֹכִיחוֹת וּמְגַנּוֹת זוֹ אֶת זוֹ בְּמִילֵי דְּעֶרְוָה.


ואת כל ונוכחת, and you will be perceived exonerated in every respect even in the eyes of those who would have felt tempted to interpret what happened to you in a negative light, reflecting discredit upon you.



### Verse 18

###### Sforno on Genesis 20:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/20:18:1)


**כִּי עָצֹר עָצַר ה' בְּעַד כָּל רֶחֶם.** לְהָמִית הַתּוֹלָדוֹת אִם לֹא הָיָה אֲבִימֶלֶךְ שָׁב בִּתְשׁוּבָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהִתְרָה בּוֹ בְּאָמְרוֹ **דַּע כִּי מוֹת תָּמוּת אַתָּה וְכָל אֲשֶׁר לָךְ**:


כי עצר עצר ה' בעד כל רחם, to kill their (unborn) offspring if Avimelech would not display remorse and penitence. G’d had warned him specifically that not only his own life was at risk (verse 7)



## Chapter 21


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:1)


**וַה' פָּקַד.** אַבְרָהָם הִתְפַּלֵּל בְּעַד אֲבִימֶלֶךְ, וַה' פָּקַד אֶת שָׂרָה, כְּאָמְרוֹ "וַה' שָׁב אֶת שְׁבוּת אִיּוֹב בְּהִתְפַּלְלוֹ בְּעַד רֵעֵהוּ" (איוב מב:י).


**Ad-noy remembered.** Literally, “*And* Ad-noy remembered” — after Avraham prayed for Avimelech, Hashem remembered Sarah.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:2)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר אָמָר.** בְּאָמְרוֹ "וּבֵרַכְתִּי אוֹתָהּ" (בראשית יז:טז), וְזֶה הָיָה שֶׁהֵסִיר מִמֶּנָּה קִלְלַת חַוָּה בְּעִבּוּר וּבְלֵידָה וּבְגִדּוּל, כְּאָמְרוֹ "הַרְבָּה אַרְבֶּה עִצְּבוֹנֵךְ וְהֵרֹנֵךְ" (בראשית ג:טז).


**As He had said.** “I will bless her” (17:16) — that is, He delivered her from the curse of Chavah (see 3:16).


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:1:3)


**וַיַּעַשׂ ה' לְשָׂרָה כַּאֲשֶׁר דִּבֵּר.** כְּאָמְרוֹ (בראשית יז:טז) "וְגַם נָתַתִּי מִמֶּנָּה לְךָ בֵּן", שֶׁלֹּא כְּמִנְהַג הַמּוֹלִידִים בְּעֵת זִקְנָה שֶׁיּוֹלִידוּ עַל הָרוֹב נְקֵבָה:


**As He had spoken.** “I will also give you a son through her” (17:16) — in defiance of the general rule that elderly parents beget daughters.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:5:1)


**וְאַבְרָהָם בֶּן מְאַת שָׁנָה.** וְעִם כָּל זֶה מָל אֶת בְּנוֹ, וְלֹא חָס עַל הֱיוֹתוֹ בֶּן זְקוּנִים.


ואברהם בן מאת שנה, although as an old man we could have expected Avraham to delegate the task of performing his son’s circumcision, he did so himself, ignoring the fact that he was an old man. [the commentary is based on the fact that we knew that Avraham was 100 years old at the time, so why repeat it?]



### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:6:1)


**צָחוֹק עָשָׂה לִי אֱלֹהִים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ צַעַר מִילָה לַתִּינוֹק, הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ נָתַן שִׂמְחָה בְּלִבִּי, לְפִיכָךְ **כָּל הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ יִצְחַק לִי** וְיִשְׂמַח בַּעֲדִי וְלֹא יִדְאַג בִּשְׁבִיל צַעַר הַמִּילָה.


צחוק עשה לי אלקים, even though my son has to endure the pain of the circumcision at such as tender age, nonetheless my heart is full of joy. Therefore, everyone who hears about this event will rejoice on my behalf ignoring the aspect of the circumcision.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:9:1)


**אֶת בֶּן הָגָר הַמִּצְרִית.** חָשְׁבָה שֶׁהִתְעוֹרֵר לָזֶה הַלַּעַג מִפְּנֵי שֶׁשָּׁמַע כָּךְ מֵאִמּוֹ, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (סוכה נו.): "שׁוּתָא דִּינוּקָא בְּשׁוּקָא אוֹ דְּאָבוּהִי אוֹ דְּאִימֵּיהּ".


את בן הגר המצרית, she assumed that the reason Ishmael had made disparaging remarks about Yitzchok was due to his mother putting him up to it, or to the Egyptian genes of his mother coming into play here. We have a saying in Sukkah 56 that the prattle of children in public reflects either what they picked up from their father or what they picked up from their mother. In the case of Ishmael, he certainly did not overhear his father make such disparaging remarks.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:9:2)


**מְצַחֵק.** מַלְעִיג עַל הַמִּשְׁתֶּה שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה בְּבֵית אַבְרָהָם, בְּאָמְרוֹ שֶׁנִּתְעַבְּרָה מֵאֲבִימֶלֶךְ. וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁלֹּא קָרָה זֶה בְּעֵת לֵידַת יִצְחָק, כִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל שָׁמַע כָּזֹאת אַחַר כָּךְ מִלֵּיצָנֵי הַדּוֹר, וְאִם בְּאוּלַי בְּעֵת הַלֵּידָה הָיָה מְצַחֵק – לֹא הִרְגִּישָׁה בּוֹ שָׂרָה שֶׁהָיְתָה אָז טְרוּדָה.


מצחק, making fun of the big party Avraham had given to mark the weaning of Yitzchok, He claimed that surely Sarah must have become pregnant from Avimelech. The reason he had not made such remarks already at the time when Yitzchok had been born, was because he had only overheard wicked gossip about this at a later stage, and now he repeated what he had heard.



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:10:1)


**גָּרֵשׁ אֶת הָאָמָה הַזֹּאת וְאֶת בְּנָהּ.** כִּי בַּעֲצָתָהּ עָשָׂה הַבֵּן שֶׁהוֹצִיא דִּבָּה לְמַעַן **יִירַשׁ** בְּנָהּ אֶת הַכֹּל, לְפִיכָךְ גָּרֵשׁ מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֵינוֹ דִּין שֶׁ**יִּירַשׁ** אֲפִלּוּ בִּקְצָת.


גרש האמה הזאת ואת בנה, seeing that what the son did was at the instigation of his mother. He spread lies about Yitzchok’s legitimacy in order to establish a claim to your inheritance.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:10:2)


**כִּי לֹא יִירַשׁ בֶּן הָאָמָה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְיֻחָס אַחֲרֶיךָ שֶׁהַוָּלָד הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַפָּגוּם:


כי לא יירש בן האמה, seeing that he is not part of your family; the child always is considered as part of the halachically defective parent. (Maimonides issurey bi-ah 15,7)



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:1)


**אַל יֵרַע בְּעֵינֶיךָ וְכוּ' כֹּל אֲשֶׁר תֹּאמַר אֵלֶיךָ שָׂרָה.** אַל יֵרַע בְּעֵינֶיךָ מַה שֶׁתֹּאמַר בִּדְבַר הַנַּעַר וְהָאָמָה לְגָרְשָׁם עִם סִימָנֵי עַבְדוּת, כְּאָמְרוֹ **עַל שִׁכְמָהּ וְכוּ'**.


אל ירע בעיניך על הנער ועל אמתך. כל אשר תאמר אליך שרה, do not be angry regarding anything that Sarah says to you concerning the lad and the maid, i.e. to expel them while they wear proof that they are slaves. Avraham’s having placed Ishmael’s on Hagar’s shoulder was a clear sign that Hagar was a slave woman who was charged with carrying Ishmael (verse 14)


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:2)


**שְׁמַע בְּקוֹלָהּ.** כִּי בְּדִין אָמְרָה לַעֲשׂוֹת כָּךְ.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:12:3)


**כִּי בְיִצְחָק יִקָּרֵא לְךָ זָרַע.** וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל וּבָנָיו יִהְיוּ עֲבָדִים לוֹ, כִּי בְיִצְחָק יִקָּרֵא לְךָ זָרַע וְלֹא בּוֹ.


כי ביצחק יקרא לך זרע, for your descendants will be known through Yitzchok, and Ishmael and his sons will be slaves to him. Ishmael will not be known historically for his being a son of Avraham.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:13:1)


**וְגַם אֶת בֶּן הָאָמָה.** וְגַם אֵין רָאוּי שֶׁתִּדְאַג עַל שֶׁתְּגָרֵשׁ אֶת הַבֵּן, כִּי תְגָרֵשׁ אֶת בֶּן הָאָמָה וְלֹא בִּנְךָ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם **לְגוֹי אֲשִׂימֶנּוּ כִּי זַרְעֲךָ הוּא**, לֹא שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הוּא רָאוּי לְכָךְ.


וגם את בן האמה, you have no reason to worry about expelling your son, as you are expelling the son of the slave woman. You do not expel him in his capacity of being your son. At any rate, I will make him into a nation seeing that he is your seed, not because he deserves such a distinction.



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:1)


**שָׂם עַל שִׁכְמָהּ.** אֶת הַ**חֵמֶת** לְאוֹת שִׁפְחוּת, עַל דֶּרֶךְ "וְהִתְעַנִּי תַּחַת יָדֶיהָ" (בראשית טז:ט), אֲבָל לֹא חָדַל הַצַּדִּיק מִתֵּת לָהֶם דֵּי מַחְסוֹרָם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה "וַיְהִי אֱלֹהִים אֶת הַנַּעַר" (בראשית כא:כ) לְרַבּוֹת חֲמָרָיו גְּמַלָּיו וּפוֹעֲלָיו, וְלָכֵן לֹא חָסְרוּ דָּבָר זוּלָתִי הַמַּיִם בִּתְעוֹתָם בַּמִּדְבָּר, וּבְהִמָּצֵא הַמַּיִם יָשַׁב שָׁם בַּמִּדְבָּר כְּטִבְעוֹ כִּי הָיָה פֶּרֶא אָדָם.


שם על שכמה, the hose containing the water; this was in order to show that she was a slave woman. A reminder of the angel at the time having told her that she was to submit to her mistress Sarah (16,9) A righteous person of the caliber of Avraham would certainly not send away a woman and her son without provisions adequate to ensure that she can reach the next location where new provisions can be obtained. [I wonder why Hagar was not given an animal to ride on? Clearly, Avraham was intent that she should be known to be a slave by the fact that she had to travel on foot. Ed.] Our sages in Bereshit Rabbah 53,15 understand the words ויהי אלוקים את הנער in verse 20, to mean that she had both donkeys and camels at her disposal as well as her son’s servants. If we accept that line in the Midrash at face value, the crisis which overtook Hagar and Ishmael was only that they ran out of water due to having lost their way in the desert. As soon as they found a source of water they had all the means to establish themselves economically, even in the midst of the desert. It was natural for Ishmael to settle in the desert as his mother had been told already while she was pregnant that here son would be a פרא אדם, someone preferring to live in the wilderness, not in civilised society.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:2)


**וְאֶת הַיֶּלֶד.** נָתַן לָהּ גַּם אֶת הַיֶּלֶד:


ואת הילד, he also gave her the child;


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:14:3)


**וַיְשַׁלְּחֶהָ.** לִוָּה אוֹתָהּ בְּרוֹב חַסְדּוֹ, כְּמוֹ "וְאַבְרָהָם הֹלֵךְ עִמָּם לְשַׁלְּחָם" (בראשית יח:טז).


וישלחה, he first accompanied her some distance out of his loving concern for them. (compare 18,16).



### Verse 19

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:19:1)


**וַיִּפְקַח אֱלֹהִים אֶת עֵינֶיהָ.** נָתַן בָּהּ דַּעַת לְהַכִּיר מְקוֹם מַיִם שֶׁהָיָה שָׁם, כִּי לֹא הָיְתָה סוּמָא קוֹדֶם לָכֵן:


ויפקח אלוקים את עיניה, He granted her the instinct to look for water in the place where she would find it. She had not been blind previously so that her eyes had to be “opened.”



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:22:1)


**אֱלֹהִים עִמְּךָ.** לְפִיכָךְ אֲנִי יָרֵא מִמְּךָ, לֹא מִגְּבוּרָתְךָ וְעָשְׁרְךָ, לָכֵן אֲנִי מְבַקֵּשׁ שֶׁתִּשָּׁבַע **לִי**:


אלוקים עמך, seeing that G’d clearly is on your side I am afraid of you. I am not afraid of your personal strength, but of that of your G’d. This is why I ask you to swear to me. [so that you cannot appeal to G’d’s assistance in any grievance you would have against me. Ed.]



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:23:1)


**כַּחֶסֶד אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתִי עִמְּךָ תַּעֲשֶׂה עִמָּדִי.** עֲשֵׂה עִמָּדִי חֶסֶד לְהִשָּׁבַע בְּעַד בָּנֶיךָ:


כחסד אשר עשיתי עמך תעשה עמדי, do me the favour to include your children in the oath you swear to me.



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:24:1)


**אָנֹכִי אִשָּׁבֵעַ.** אָנֹכִי אֶעֱשֶׂה הַחֶסֶד לְהִשָּׁבַע, אֲבָל אַתָּה לֹא עָשִׂיתָ עִמָּדִי חֶסֶד כִּדְבָרֶיךָ, שֶׁעֲבָדֶיךָ גְּזָלוּנִי.


אנכי אשבע, I will do you the favour to swear such an oath; however, you have not done me any favours as you claim to have done.



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:25:1)


**אֲשֶׁר גָּזְלוּ עַבְדֵי אֲבִימֶלֶךְ.** הוֹכִיחוֹ בַּאֲשֶׁר הוּא מוֹשֵׁל עַל הַגֶּזֶל הַנַּעֲשֶׂה בְּאַרְצוֹ וְאֵין מַכְלִים, וְהוֹכִיחוֹ גַּם כֵּן עַל הָרְשָׁעִים שֶׁהָיוּ בְּבֵיתוֹ שֶׁלֹּא כְּדֶרֶךְ הַצַּדִּיקִים, כְּאָמְרָם "לֹא יֵשֵׁב בְּקֶרֶב בֵּיתִי עֹשֵׂה רְמִיָּה" (תהלים קא:ז).


אשר גזלו עבדי אבימלך, he criticised him precisely in his position of king, seeing that robbery had been tolerated in his country and there was no one to protest this. He also criticised him for the evildoers in his own entourage. He did not act according to the norms of righteous people. Psalms 101,7 suggests that righteous people have no business dwelling in the proximity of people who practice deception.



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:26:1)


**לֹא יָדַעְתִּי מִי עָשָׂה.** גַּם עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁהוֹדַעְתַּנִי כִּי נַעֲשָׂה הַגָּזֵל עַל יְדֵי עֲבָדַי, לֹא יָדַעְתִּי לְשַׁעֵר מִי מֵהֶם עָשָׂה זֶה, כִּי אֵין שׁוּם אֶחָד מֵהֶם חָשׁוּד אֶצְלִי, שֶׁאִם הָיָה חָשׁוּד לֹא הָיָה יוֹשֵׁב בְּבֵיתִי.


לא ידעתי מי עשה, even now that you have informed me by means of my servants that you have been unjustly victimised, I still do not know who are the ones who could have done this. Not a single one among the people concerned seems suspect to me. Had I suspected any one of my servants of such conduct he would not have been tolerated in my entourage.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:26:2)


**וְגַם אַתָּה לֹא הִגַּדְתָּ לִּי וְגַם אָנֹכִי לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי.** עַל מָה שֶׁהוֹכַחְתַּנִי שֶׁבִּהְיוֹתִי מֶלֶךְ עָלַי הָיָה לְאַשֵּׁר חָמוֹץ, הִנֵּה הַמֶּלֶךְ לֹא יַעֲשֶׂה זֶה כִּי אִם עַל אֶחָד מִשְּׁנֵי פָנִים: אִם לְקוֹל צַעֲקַת הַנִּגְזָל, וְאִם לְקוֹל הָמוֹן מְפַרְסֵם אֵיזֶה גֶּזֶל שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה. וְהִנֵּה אַתָּה לֹא הִגַּדְתָּ לִי וְגַם אָנֹכִי לֹא שָׁמַעְתִּי קוֹל הָמוֹן בָּזֶה.


וגם אתה לא הגדת לי וגם אנכי לא שמעתי, what you have accused me of, that while in my position as king I had condoned violence. A king does interfere such matters only if one of two conditions exist 1) If the party that has suffered robbery complains loudly; 2) if neutral people aware of the robbery raise their voices in protest. In the case of Avraham, neither of such conditions existed until now.



### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:30:1)


**כִּי אֶת שֶׁבַע כְּבָשׂוֹת תִּקַּח מִיָּדִי.** עַל דֶּרֶךְ "שָׁלַף אִישׁ נַעֲלוֹ וְנָתַן לְרֵעֵהוּ וְזֹאת הַתְּעוּדָה" (רות ד:ז) כִּי הָיָה זֶה עֵדוּת הַסְכָּמַת הַבְּעָלִים וְהוֹדָאָתָם בַּדָּבָר.


כי את שבע כבשות תקח מידי, this is similar to the procedure described in Ruth 4,7 known as חליצה, in which the widow of the deceased brother who has been refused levirate marriage by her brother-in-law removed a shoe from her brother-in-law. This transaction signaled mutual agreement. In the case of חליצה it signaled that the widow was now free to marry someone of her choice instead of being beholden to a surviving brother-in-law.


###### Sforno on Genesis 21:30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:30:2)


**בַּעֲבוּר תִּהְיֶה לִּי** הַבְּרִית **לְעֵדָה** עַל הוֹדָאָתְךָ **כִּי חָפַרְתִּי אֶת הַבְּאֵר הַזֹּאת**.


In this instance, acceptance of the gift meant that Avraham had dug the well, owned it, as well as the land it was on.



### Verse 32

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:32:1)


**וַיָּשׁוּבוּ אֶל אֶרֶץ פְּלִשְׁתִּים.** שָׁבוּ מִבְּאֵר שֶׁבַע שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בְּאֶרֶץ פְּלִשְׁתִּים, שֶׁהָלְכוּ שָׁם לְדַבֵּר עִם אַבְרָהָם שֶׁהָלַךְ שָׁם לִרְאוֹת מִקְנֵהוּ, וְשָׁם נָתַן צֹאן וּבָקָר וְכִבְשׂוֹת לִכְרוֹת הַבְּרִית.


וישובו אל ארץ פלשתים, they returned from Beer Sheva, i.e. proof that Beer Sheva was not part of the land of the Philistines. They had gone there only to speak with Avraham who had gone there to see how his livestock was faring. This is where he tendered the gift to confirm the covenant they had made.



### Verse 33

###### Sforno on Genesis 21:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/21:33:1)


**וַיִּקְרָא שָׁם בְּשֵׁם ה' אֵל עוֹלָם.** וְקָרָא וְהוֹדִיעַ לָרַבִּים שֶׁהָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה הוּא אֱלֹהֵי הַזְּמַן וּמַמְצִיאוֹ, נֶגֶד דַּעַת אֶפִּיקוֹרְסִים חֲדָשִׁים גַּם יְשָׁנִים:


ויקרא שם בשם ה' א-ל עולם, he used the occasion to proclaim the eternal nature of G’d, as opposed to any other deities, which, even if on occasion appearing to have demonstrated some power to protect those worshipping them, could at best enjoy a brief period of effectiveness.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:1:1)


**נִסָּה אֶת אַבְרָהָם.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בְּפֹעַל אוֹהֵב וְיָרֵא כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה בַּכֹּחַ, וּבָזֶה יִדְמֶה יוֹתֵר לְבוֹרְאוֹ שֶׁהוּא טוֹב לָעוֹלָם בְּפֹעַל, כִּי אָמְנָם הַכַּוָּנָה בִּמְצִיאוּת הָאָדָם הָיְתָה שֶׁיִּדְמֶה לְבוֹרְאוֹ כְּפִי הָאֶפְשָׁר, כַּאֲשֶׁר הֵעִיד בְּאָמְרוֹ "נַעֲשֶׂה אָדָם בְּצַלְמֵנוּ כִּדְמוּתֵנוּ" (בראשית א:כו).


נסה את אברהם, to demonstrate that Avraham’s love for G’d as well as his fear of G’d was not merely potential but actual, much as G’d's goodness is not merely potential but actual. The purpose of man’s existence is to emulate the virtues of G’d, and by means of this “test” Avraham had an opportunity to demonstrate this. When G’d created man He had set Himself the objective of “let us make man in Our image etc.,” i.e. as much like Divine beings as is it possible for a creature to be . (Genesis 1,26)



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:3:1)


**וַיֵּלֶךְ אֶל הַמָּקוֹם.** אֶל אֶרֶץ הַמּוֹרִיָּה:


וילך אל המקום, to the land of Moriah.



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:4:1)


**וַיַּרְא אֶת הַמָּקוֹם.** מְקוֹם הַזֶּבַח בְּהַר הַמּוֹרִיָּה:


וישא אברהם את עיניו וירא את המקום, the place for offering the sacrifice on the mountain of Moriah.


###### Sforno on Genesis 22:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:4:2)


**מֵרָחוֹק.** וְשָׁלְטָה בּוֹ עֵינוֹ לִרְאוֹת מָקוֹם מֵרָחוֹק בִּרְצוֹן הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ, כְּעִנְיַן "וַיַּרְאֵהוּ ה' אֶת כָּל הָאָרֶץ" (דברים לד:א), וְהֵבִין שֶׁבְּאוֹתוֹ הַמָּקוֹם יִהְיֶה מְקוֹם הַזֶּבַח.


מרחוק, he was granted additional eyesight to espy this place from a greater distance than he would normally have been able to see.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:5:1)


**שְׁבוּ לָכֶם פֹּה.** כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יִמְחוּ בּוֹ וְשֶׁלֹּא יַטְרִידוּהוּ בַּעֲשׂוֹתוֹ הַזֶּבַח.


שבו לכם פה, so that they should not be able to interfere with what he was about to do.



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:12:1)


**עַתָּה יָדַעְתִּי.** יָדַעְתִּי אֲנִי הַמַּלְאָךְ שֶׁבְּדִין יַגְדִּילְךָ הָאֵל עַל מַלְאָכָיו, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (סנהדרין צג): "גְּדוֹלִים צַדִּיקִים יוֹתֵר מִמַּלְאֲכֵי הַשָּׁרֵת":


**Now I know.** These were the words of the angel — “Now I know why God elevated you above us.”


###### Sforno on Genesis 22:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:12:2)


**מִמֶּנִּי.** שֶׁאַתָּה **יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים** יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנִּי שֶׁאֲנִי מַלְאָךְ וְרָאוּי לְמַעֲלָה יוֹתֵר מִמֶּנִּי, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה "גְּדוֹלִים צַדִּיקִים וְכוּ'". שֶׁאַתָּה בְּפֹעַל **יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים** כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה הָאֵל יוֹדֵעַ קוֹדֶם לָכֵן שֶׁהָיִיתָ יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים בְּכֹחַ, וְתִפּוֹל יְדִיעָתוֹ הַפּוֹעֶלֶת עַל הַנִּמְצָא בְּפֹעַל.


**From Me (or, “than I am”).** These are also the words of the angel. The verse should be inverted and read: “You are more God-fearing than I am because you did not withhold your son”.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:13:1)


**וְהִנֵּה אַיִל אַחַר נֶאֱחַז בַּסְּבַךְ.** וּבָזֶה הֵבִין שֶׁנִּזְדַּמֵּן לוֹ הָאַיִל בִּרְצוֹן הָאֵל, וְשֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בּוֹ שׁוּם חֲשַׁשׁ שֶׁל גָּזֵל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁרָאָה שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה שָׁם אַיִל קוֹדֵם לָכֵן, וְאַחַר זֶה תֵּכֶף רָאָה זֶה הָאַיִל נֶאֱחָז בַּסְּבָךְ:


והנה איל אחר נאחז בסבך, this incident indicated to him that G’d must have arranged for this ram to be at his disposal, and that there could be no question of his committing robbery if he took this ram and sacrificed it as a burnt offering. The fact that there had been no such ram when he prepared the altar and bound Yitzchok and suddenly this ram materialised, was ample evidence of its purpose.


###### Sforno on Genesis 22:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:13:2)


**תַּחַת בְּנוֹ.** תְּמוּרַת מַה שֶּׁהָיָה בְּלִבּוֹ לִזְבֹּחַ אֶת בְּנוֹ, עַל דֶּרֶךְ "וְדֹבֵר אֱמֶת בִּלְבָבוֹ" (תהלים טו:ב).


תחת בנו, in exchange for what he had had in mind to do with his son. The phrase reminds us of Psalms 15,2 ודובר אמת בלבו, “he spoke truthfully in his heart.” [the author justifies the use of the word תחת, “in lieu of,” for something which had not actually happened, i.e. Yitzchok had not been slaughtered. The verse from Psalms proves that the intention is as good as the deed when such an intention was sincere, wholehearted. Ed.]



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:14:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יֵאָמֵר הַיּוֹם.** הַמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָיוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל אוֹמְרִים בְּיוֹם נְתִינַת הַתּוֹרָה שֶׁ**בְּהַר ה' יֵרָאֶה**, כַּאֲשֶׁר יְגַלֶּה אוֹתוֹ הָאֵל יִתְעַלֶּה בְּאָמְרוֹ "וְהָיָה הַמָּקוֹם אֲשֶׁר יִבְחַר" (דברים יב:יא), וְזֶה הָיָה בִּימֵי דָּוִד. אוֹתוֹ הַמָּקוֹם קָרָא אוֹתוֹ **אַבְרָהָם ה' יִרְאֶה**.


אשר יאמר היום, the place of which the Israelites had said on the day that the Torah was written (or given) that G’d manifests Himself on the mountain. G’d would reveal which mountain this is only in the future, in the days of David, seeing that even in Deuteronomy 12,11 only veiled reference is made to such a mountain. It was this mountain, subsequently known as the Temple Mount which Avraham now named ה' יראה.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:16:1)


**נְאֻם ה' כִּי יַעַן אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתָ.** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי ה' כִּי יַעַן אֲשֶׁר עָשִׂיתָ זֶה:


נאם ה' כי יען אשר עשית, “I, G’d, say that because you have done this I will bless you in an increased measure.”



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:17:1)


**כִּי בָרֵךְ אֲבָרֶכְךָ.** בִּי נִשְׁבַּעְתִּי שֶׁאֲבָרֵךְ אוֹתְךָ:


בי נשבעתי, “I have sworn to Myself that I will greatly bless you/ you.”



### Verse 18

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:18:1)


**וְהִתְבָּרְכוּ בְזַרְעֲךָ כֹּל גּוֹיֵי הָאָרֶץ.** כְּשֶׁיִּקְרְאוּ כֻּלָּם בְּשֵׁם ה' לְעָבְדוֹ שְׁכֶם אֶחָד, כֻּלָּם יִתְבָּרְכוּ בְּזַרְעֲךָ וְיִשְׁתַּדְּלוּ לְהִדָּמוֹת לָהֶם:


והתברכו בזרעך כל גויי הארץ, if and when your descendants will join together in proclaiming the holy name of the Lord, the nations of the world will be blessed as they will try to follow your example and to be like you.


###### Sforno on Genesis 22:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:18:2)


**עֵקֶב אֲשֶׁר שָׁמַעְתָּ בְּקוֹלִי.** שֶׁשְּׂכַר מִצְוָה מִצְוָה, שֶׁתִּזְכֶּה בָּזֶה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ נְבִיאֵי בָּנֶיךָ לְנֵס עַמִּים, מוֹרִים לַגּוֹיִם עֲבוֹדַת הָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ, וּלְךָ תִּהְיֶה צְדָקָה.


עקב אשר שמעת בקולי, as a consequence. This is what we know as the concept of שכר מצוה מצוה, that the fulfillment of one commandment begets the opportunity of fulfilling another commandment. By doing this you will receive the satisfaction that your children will be a banner to the nations, teachers to the gentile nations instructing them in how to serve the Lord. All of this will accrue to you as a credit, a merit.



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:20:1)


**הִנֵּה יָלְדָה מִלְכָּה.** הִנֵּה כְּבָר יָדַעְתָּ שֶׁמִּלְכָּה יָלְדָה בָּנִים:


**She also gave birth.** Avraham was told that if he did not wish to take Rivkah for his son, Re’umah also had a daughter — Ma’achoh — who was fitting to be Yitzchok’s wife.


###### Sforno on Genesis 22:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:20:2)


**גַם הִיא.** מִלְּבַד הַפִּילֶגֶשׁ:


גם היא, in addition to the children born by his concubine.



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:23:1)


**וּבְתוּאֵל יָלַד אֶת רִבְקָה.** וְהִגִּיד הַמַּגִּיד שֶׁבְּתוּאֵל, שֶׁהָיָה מִבְּנֵי הַגְּבִירָה, יָלַד אֶת רִבְקָה, וְהָיְתָה הַבְּשׂוֹרָה שֶׁיִּמְצָא בְּבֵית אָבִיו אִשָּׁה לִבְנוֹ וְלֹא יִצְטָרֵךְ לִדְבֹּק בְּכַנְעַן.


ובתואל ילד את רבקה, the person relating all this to Avraham added that Betuel, a son of Nachor’s proper wife, sired Rivkah. The meaning of the message was that there had been born in his own family a girl who would be a suitable wife for Avraham’s son so that he would not have to look for such a wife among the Canaanites surrounding him.



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/22:24:1)


**וּפִילַגְשׁוֹ וַתֵּלֶד גַּם הִיא.** וְהִגִּיד הַמַּגִּיד שֶׁגַּם פִּילַגְשׁוֹ יָלְדָה **אֶת מַעֲכָה**, שֶׁהָיְתָה כְּמוֹ כֵן רְאוּיָה לִבְנוֹ אִם לֹא יִבְחַר בְּרִבְקָה וְלֹא יִצְטָרֵךְ לְזֶרַע כְּנָעַן:



## Chapter 23


### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:2:1)


**וַתָּמָת שָׂרָה.** אַחַר שֶׁנּוֹלְדָה רִבְקָה הָרְאוּיָה לְמַלֵּאת מְקוֹם שָׂרָה, וְשֶׁנּוֹדַע זֶה לְאַבְרָהָם, מֵתָה שָׂרָה. כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (יומא לח.) שֶׁאֵין צַדִּיק נִפְטָר מִן הָעוֹלָם אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן נוֹלַד צַדִּיק כְּמוֹתוֹ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (קהלת א:ה) "וְזָרַח הַשֶּׁמֶשׁ וּבָא הַשָּׁמֶשׁ".


**Sarah died.** She did not die until a fitting successor was born and Avraham was apprised of the fact.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:2:2)


**לִסְפֹּד לְשָׂרָה.** בִּשְׁבִיל שָׂרָה וְלִכְבוֹדָהּ, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (סַנְהֶדְרִין מו:) הֶסְפֵּידָא יְקָרָא דְּשָׁכְבֵי.


**To eulogize Sarah.** Literally, “to eulogize *for* Sarah” — a eulogy is for the honor of the deceased.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:3:1)


**מֵעַל פְּנֵי מֵתוֹ וַיְדַבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי חֵת.** שֶׁלֹּא חָל עָלָיו אֲבֵלוּת עֲדַיִן, וְהָיָה יָכוֹל לָצֵאת וּלְדַבֵּר אֶל בְּנֵי חֵת.


מעל פני מתו, וידבר אל בני חת. As long as he had not buried Sarah he was not yet a mourner in the technical meaning of the word. He was therefore able to leave his house and assemble the people of the town.



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:4:1)


**גֵּר וְתוֹשָׁב.** וּבִשְׁבִיל הֱיוֹתִי גֵּר אֵין לִי פֹּה קֶבֶר, כְּאָמְרוֹ "וּמִי לְךָ פֹה כִּי חָצַבְתָּ לְּךָ פֹּה קָבֶר" (ישעיהו כב:טז), וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁאֲנִי מְבַקֵּשׁ לִקְנוֹתוֹ הוּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאֲנִי תוֹשָׁב עִמָּכֶם, שֶׁבָּאתִי לְהִשְׁתַּקֵּעַ לֹא לָגוּר בִּלְבָד:


גר ותושב, because I used to be a stranger here, I do not possess an ancestral burial ground. The word is used in this sense by Isaiah 22,16 ומי לך פה כי חצבת לך פה קבר, “whom have you here that you have hewn out a tomb for yourself here?”


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:4:2)


**תְּנוּ לִי אֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר.** הִסְכִּימוּ שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לִי אֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר, כְּאָמְרוֹ **לְאַבְרָהָם לַאֲחֻזַּת קָבֶר מֵאֵת בְּנֵי חֵת**.


תנו לי אחוזת קבר, “be agreeable to that I should possess a burial plot among you.”



### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:6:1)


**בְּמִבְחַר קְבָרֵינוּ קְבוֹר.** וְאַל תַּשְׁהֶה מֵתְךָ עַד שֶׁתִּקְנֶה, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (מועד קטן כב.) כָּל הַמֵּתִים כֻּלָּם כָּל הַמַּדְחֶה מִטָּתָם הֲרֵי זֶה מְשֻׁבָּח.


במבחר קברנו קבור, “do not wait until the formalities of the purchase have been completed, but go ahead and bury Sarah.” This corresponds to a statement by our sages in Moed Katan 22 על כל המתים כולן מדחה מטתו, “it behooves us to bury all the dead with dispatch, without undue delay.”



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:7:1)


**וַיִּשְׁתַּחוּ לְעַם הָאָרֶץ לִבְנֵי חֵת.** הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה לְאוֹתָם רָאשֵׁי הָעָם הַנּוֹעָדִים, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵם הָיוּ בִּמְקוֹם כָּל הַצִּבּוּר.


וישתחו לעם הארץ לבני חת. He bowed to the notables present, seeing they represented the whole population.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:1)


**אִם יֵשׁ אֶת נַפְשְׁכֶם לִקְבּוֹר.** אִם רְצוֹנְכֶם לִקְבּוֹר אֶת הַמֵּת מְהֵרָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֶרְאֵיתֶם בְּאָמְרְכֶם **בְּמִבְחַר קְבָרֵינוּ קְבוֹר**, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא אַשְׁהֶא:


אם יש את נפשכם לקבור, if you want that I shall bury the dead without delay, as you have indicated when you said: במבחר קברנו קבור!" (verse 6) i.e. that I would not need to wait.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:2)


**שְׁמָעוּנִי.** לְהַסְכִּים שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לִי **אֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר**:


שמעוני, agree with me that I shall receive an inalienable burial plot.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:8:3)


**וּפִגְעוּ לִי בְּעֶפְרוֹן.** שֶׁיִּמְכּוֹר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נָאוֹת לְאִישׁ נִכְבָּד לִמְכּוֹר מֵאֲחֻזָּתוֹ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהֵעִיד נָבוֹת כְּאָמְרוֹ (מלכים א כא:ג): "חָלִילָה לִי מִתִּתִּי אֶת נַחֲלַת אֲבוֹתַי לָךְ".


ופגעו לי בעפרון, that he will sell me this cave even though it is not an agreeable thing for a highly placed person to sell any portion of his land-holdings. We know this from Navot (Kings I 21,3) who had been aghast at the suggestion of selling his vineyard to the king. His precise words were: חלילה לי מתתי את נחלת אבותי לך, “far be it from me to give you any part of my ancestral heritage.”



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:1)


**מְעָרַת הַמַּכְפֵּלָה.** מָקוֹם שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ מַכְפֵּלָה, כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיָּקָם שְׂדֵה עֶפְרוֹן אֲשֶׁר בַּמַּכְפֵּלָה**.


מערת המכפלה, a place known as machpela.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:2)


**אֲשֶׁר בִּקְצֵה שָׂדֵהוּ.** וְלֹא בִּשְׁבִיל זֶה יַשְׁחִית נַחֲלָתוֹ.


Compare verse 17 ויקם שדה עפרון אשר במכפלה אשר בקצה שדהו, “Efron’s field which is situated in machpela at the edge of his field, stood, etc.” The details provided by the Torah are intended to prove to us, the reader, that by selling the cave to Avraham Efron did not suffer any loss of face, he had no reason to feel embarrassed about what he had done.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:3)


**בְּכֶסֶף מָלֵא.** שֶׁאֵין חֶפְצוֹ שֶׁיִּגְרַע מֵעֶרְכּוֹ לִכְבוֹדְכֶם.


בכסף מלא, I do not want that he grant me a reduction from its real worth as a gesture to you.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:4)


**בְּתוֹכְכֶם.** בְּמַעֲמַד כֻּלְּכֶם, שֶׁאֵינִי מְבַקֵּשׁ שׁוּם זְמַן לִפְרֹעַ אֵלָיו הַמָּעוֹת, וְכֵן עָשָׂה כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיִּשְׁקֹל אַבְרָהָם לְעֶפְרוֹן אֶת הַכֶּסֶף כּוּ' לְעֵינֵי בְנֵי חֵת**.


בתוככם, in the presence of all of you. I do not ask for time to come up with the payment but am willing to pay cash at once. Compare verse 16 where Avraham does in fact pay cash immediately.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:9:5)


**לַאֲחֻזַּת קָבֶר.** שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיִּמְכֹּר, אוּלַי יְעַרְעֵר שֶׁלֹּא אֶקְבּוֹר אֵצֶל גְּבוּלוֹ, לָכֵן אֲנִי שׁוֹאֵל שֶׁיִּמְכְּרֶנָּה לַאֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר.


לאחוזת קבר, Avraham wanted complete freedom to do what he wanted to in the property once he had acquired it. This is the privilege granted to anyone owning an ancestral heritage. Avraham wanted it understood that as a result of the sale Efron would not be able to dictate to him how close to his own property Sarah or other members of his family could be buried.



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:1)


**לֹא אֲדֹנִי.** אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּפְגְּעוּ בִּי רָאשֵׁי הָעָם הַנּוֹעָדִים:


**No, my master, hear me.** There is no need for the leaders of the community to intercede with me.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:2)


**שְׁמָעֵנִי.** אַתָּה בִּלְתִּי שׁוּם אֶמְצָעִי:


Listen to me;


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:3)


**הַשָּׂדֶה נָתַתִּי לָךְ.** בְּמַחֲשַׁבְתִּי מֵאָז שֶׁדִּבַּרְתָּ:


**I have given you the field.** In my mind I gave it to you the moment you spoke.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:4)


**וְהַמְּעָרָה אֲשֶׁר בּוֹ.** שֶׁאֵין רָאוּי שֶׁתַּעֲבוֹר בְּשָׂדֶה אֲחֵרִים כְּשֶׁתִּרְצֶה לִקְבּוֹר:


והמערה אשר בו, “and the cave which is in it, since it is not proper that you should have to cross someone else’s property in order to get to the grave of your wife. Therefore,


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:11:5)


**לְעֵינֵי בְנֵי עַמִּי נְתַתִּיהָ לָּךְ קְבֹר מֵתֶךָ.** לְעֵינֵיהֶם אֲנִי אוֹמֵר שֶׁאֲנִי נוֹתֵן לְךָ עַל מְנָת שֶׁתּוּכַל לִקְבּוֹר שָׁם מֵתְךָ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁאַלְתָּ בְּאָמְרְךָ **לַאֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר**:


לעיני בני עמי נתתיה לך קבור מתך, I am telling you in the presence of all my people that I have given it to you in order that you may bury your dead there, just as you asked when you referred to אחוזת קבר.”



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:12:1)


**וַיִּשְׁתַּחוּ אַבְרָהָם לִפְנֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ.** הִשְׁתַּחֲוָה לָתֵת לָהֶם הוֹדָאָה, כְּמוֹדֶה שֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל כְּבוֹדָם הִסְכִּים עֶפְרוֹן לָתֵת אֶת שְׁאֵלָתוֹ, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (קדושין לא.) הַנִּשְׁמָע בִּדְבַר אָבִיו לַמָּקוֹם, לֹא יֹאמַר מַהֲרוּנִי בִּשְׁבִיל עַצְמִי אֶלָּא מַהֲרוּנִי בִּשְׁבִיל אַבָּא, פָּטְרוּנִי בִּשְׁבִיל אַבָּא:


**Avraham bowed.** He bowed to them to acknowledge that it was in their honor that Ephron consented.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:13:1)


**אַךְ אִם כּוּ' נָתַתִּי כֶּסֶף הַשָּׂדֶה.** אָנֹכִי אֶעֱשֶׂה כִּדְבָרְךָ לִקְבּוֹר שָׁם הַמֵּת, אַךְ אִם נָתַתִּי כֶּסֶף הַשָּׂדֶה, וְלֹא אֶקְבּוֹר בּוֹ בְּאוֹפֶן אַחֵר.


אך אם ...נתתי בסף השדה, I will comply with what you said, namely to bury my dead there only if I can hand over the money for the field. I will not bury Sarah there as a result of any other kind of arrangement.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:13:2)


**אַתָּה לוּ שְׁמָעֵנִי.** אַתָּה בְּבַקָּשָׁה שְׁמָעֵנִי, קַח מִמֶּנִּי **וְאֶקְבְּרָה** אַחַר כָּךְ:


אך אם אתה לו שמעני, “if only you would be good enough to listen to me! Please accept the money from me so that I can proceed with the burial.”



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:15:1)


**מַה הִיא.** הֲלֹא מִצְעָר הוּא, וּמַסְפִּיק הַדִּבּוּר לְהַקְנוֹת לְךָ מֵעַכְשָׁיו, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁתִּהְיֶה קוֹבֵר בְּתוֹךְ שֶׁלְּךָ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נָתַתָּ הַמָּעוֹת עֲדַיִן:


?מה היא, “after all it is a very minor transaction! It is so insignificant a matter that the acquisition can be made by a mere declaration without being recorded in a document. As soon as you have handed over the money you may consider yourself as burying Sarah in your property.”



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:16:1)


**וַיִּשְׁמַע אַבְרָהָם לְעֶפְרוֹן.** הִסְכִּים לְדַעְתּוֹ בְּעֵרֶךְ הַשָּׂדֶה.


(1) AVRAHAM HEARKENED TO EFRON. He accepted his view of the value of the field.


###### Sforno on Genesis 23:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:16:2)


**וַיִּשְׁקֹל.** פָּרַע כְּמוֹ "אוֹ כִּכַּר כֶּסֶף תִּשְׁקוֹל" (מלכים א כ:לט).


(2) AND HE WEIGHED OUT [JPS: PAID]. He paid off, as (I Kings 20:39), "or you will have to pay a talent of silver."



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:17:1)


**וַיָּקָם הַשָּׂדֶה לְאַבְרָהָם לְמִקְנָה.** נִתְקַיֵּם בְּחוֹתָמָיו שֶׁל סֵפֶר הַמִּקְנָה.


ויקם השדה לאברהם למקנה, the word ויקם teaches that the purchase was confirmed by a sealed document



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Genesis 23:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/23:20:1)


**מֵאֵת בְּנֵי חֵת.** שֶׁהִסְכִּים כָּל הַצִּבּוּר שֶׁתִּהְיֶה לוֹ לַאֲחֻזַּת קֶבֶר.


מאת בני חת ויקם השדה, these people all agreed to the transaction.



## Chapter 24


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:1:1)


**וְאַבְרָהָם זָקֵן וַה' בֵּרַךְ.** בֵּאֵר הַסִּבּוֹת שֶׁהִכְרִיחוּ אֶת אַבְרָהָם לִשְׁלֹחַ אֶת עַבְדּוֹ אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת בְּעַד אִשָּׁה לִבְנוֹ, וְשֶׁהֻצְרַךְ לְהַשְׁבִּיעוֹ עָלָיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי לְסִבַּת הַזִּקְנָה דָּאַג שֶׁיָּמוּת וְלֹא יִרְאֶה בְּחֻפַּת בְּנוֹ, וְהִסְכִּים שֶׁלֹּא לְאַחֵר עוֹד. וּמִבְּלִי אֵין בְּאַרְצוֹ אִשָּׁה הֲגוּנָה לִבְנוֹ, הֻצְרַךְ לִשְׁלֹחַ אֶל אֶרֶץ אַחֶרֶת. וּמִפְּנֵי עָשְׁרוֹ דָּאַג שֶׁמָּא אֵיזֶה אָדָם בִּלְתִּי הָגוּן יַרְבֶּה שֹׁחַד לְעַבְדּוֹ כְּדֵי שֶׁיִּבְחַר בְּבִתּוֹ, וְלֹא יִשְׁתַּדֵּל לְהַשִּׂיג אִשָּׁה הֲגוּנָה לִבְנוֹ. וְלָכֵן הֻצְרַךְ לְהַשְׁבִּיעַ אֶת עַבְדּוֹ עַל כָּל אֵלֶּה.


**Avraham was old.** He was concerned that he might die without seeing his son married off; therefore he resolved to seek a wife for him without delay. **Place your hand.** Because of his wealth Avraham was concerned that someone unworthy might bribe Eliezer into taking his daughter for Yitzchok. For this reason he made him swear not to take a Canaanite woman.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:3:1)


**אֱלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם וֵאלֹהֵי הָאָרֶץ.** שֶׁאִם תִּבְגּוֹד יִפָּרַע מִמְּךָ בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.


אלוקי השמים ואלוקי הארץ, a veiled warning to Eliezer that if he were to betray his trust, there would be a higher power to hold him accountable for such a betrayal.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:5:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יָצָאתָ מִשָּׁם.** שֶׁמָּאַסְתָּ אֶת הַמָּקוֹם, וְהִנֵּה אִם אֶשָּׁבַע לָקַחַת אִשָּׁה מִשָּׁם לֹא תַחְפֹּץ הִיא לָבֹא הֵנָּה, אֶשְׁעַבֵּד אֶת בִּנְךָ לִשְׁאֵר כְּסוּת וְעוֹנָה, וְיִתְחַיֵּב לָלֶכֶת שָׁם, וְאִם לֹא יֵלֵךְ יִבְגֹּד בְּאֵשֶׁת נְעוּרָיו:


אשר יצאת משם, a place which you had despised. If I now swear to you to take a wife from there and the girl I choose refuses to come here, my betrothing her to your son will obligate him to fulfill his conjugal duties including living with her as husband and wife. If he refuses to join her there he will have become guilty of betrayal of his bride. Shall I really bring him back there?



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:1)


**אֱלֹהֵי הַשָּׁמַיִם יִשְׁלַח מַלְאָכוֹ.** יְהִי רָצוֹן שֶׁיִּשְׁלַח מַלְאָכוֹ מִמְּעוֹן קָדְשׁוֹ הַשָּׁמַיִם, בִּהְיוֹת שֶׁהוּא לְקָחַנִי מִשָּׁם, וְלוֹ נִתְכְּנוּ עֲלִילוֹת לַעֲשׂוֹת בְּאוֹפֶן שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטָרֵךְ בְּנִי לָלֶכֶת שָׁם:


אלוקי השמים...ישלח מלאכו, may it be the will of the Lord that He will send His angel from the celestial regions. Seeing that it was he Who had taken me from there, and all events are under His constant supervision, He will not arrange matters in such a way that my son would be legally obligated to migrate to that land.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:2)


**וַאֲשֶׁר דִּבֶּר לִי.** וְעוֹד כִּי דִּבֶּר לִי בְּאָמְרוֹ "כִּי בְיִצְחָק יִקָּרֵא לְךָ זָרַע" (בראשית כא:יב).


ואשר דבר לי, furthermore, I remember that he assured me specifically that my offspring will become known as offspring of Avraham though my son Yitzchok.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:7:3)


**וַאֲשֶׁר נִשְׁבַּע לִי.** וְכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּע וְלֹא יִנָּחֵם, יְסַבֵּב בְּלִי סָפֵק שֶׁיִּשָּׂא בְּנִי אִשָּׁה הוֹגֶנֶת לוֹ לְהָקִים זֶרַע מוּכָן לְקַיֵּם בּוֹ שְׁבוּעָתוֹ:


ואשר נשבע לי, seeing that He made me this promise reinforced by an oath, I am certain that He will not renege on it. [even if I were to fall out of favour in His eyes due to my own fault. Ed.] He will see to it that my son will be able to marry a suitable soul-mate and thus make His oath come true.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:9:1)


**וַיִּשָּׁבַע לוֹ עַל הַדָּבָר הַזֶּה.** נִשְׁבַּע עִם כָּל הַתְּנָאִים שֶׁאָמַר לוֹ אַבְרָהָם:


וישבע לו על הדבר הזה, he accepted all of Avraham’s conditions as part of his oath.



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:1)


**וַיִּקַּח הָעֶבֶד כּוּ' וַיֵּלֶךְ.** נָטַל רְשׁוּת מֵאֲדוֹנָיו לָלֶכֶת, אַחַר שֶׁהֵכִין אֶת הַגְּמַלִּים, וַיֵּלֶךְ מִלִּפְנֵי אֲדוֹנָיו.


ויקח העבד עשרה גמלים מדמלי אדוניו וילך. He took permission from his master to set out on the way after he made prepared the camels for the journey.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:2)


**וְכָל טוּב אֲדוֹנָיו בְּיָדוֹ.** וְנָטַל עִמּוֹ **כְּלֵי כֶסֶף וּכְלֵי זָהָב וּבְגָדִים**, וְלֹא הֻצְרַךְ בָּזֶה לִטּוֹל רְשׁוּת כִּי הַכֹּל הָיָה בְּיָדוֹ לַעֲשׂוֹת בּוֹ כִּרְצוֹנוֹ.


וכל טוב אדוניו בידו, he took with him many silver and golden trinkets. He did not need to ask for permission to do this seeing that all of these items were under his authority on an all year round basis in his capacity of being Avraham’s general manager.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:10:3)


**וַיָּקָם וַיֵּלֶךְ.** לְדַרְכּוֹ.


ויקם וילך, on his way.



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:1)


**וְהָיָה הַנַּעֲרָה אֲשֶׁר אֹמַר אֵלֶיהָ.** הִתְפַּלֵּל שֶׁיִּהְיֶה כָּךְ, לֹא שֶׁסָּמַךְ עַל נַחַשׁ, וְכֵן הָיָה בִּיהוֹנָתָן בֶּן שָׁאוּל (שמואל א יד:י), וּמַה שֶׁאָמְרוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה ״כָּל נַחַשׁ שֶׁאֵינוֹ כְּאֱלִיעֶזֶר עֶבֶד אַבְרָהָם וִיהוֹנָתָן בֶּן שָׁאוּל אֵינוֹ נַחַשׁ״ (חולין צה.), הַכַּוָּנָה הִיא שֶׁיֹּאמַר הַמְנַחֵשׁ כְּדִבְרֵיהֶם, אֲבָל יֹאמַר זֶה שֶׁלֹּא בְּדֶרֶךְ תְּפִלָּה אֶלָּא דֶּרֶךְ נַחַשׁ, שֶׁאִם יִקְרֶה כֵּן יַעֲשֶׂה כָּךְ וְכָךְ:


והיה הנערה אשר אמר אליה, this was not a statement but a prayer that things should develop in this fashion. He did not rely on any magic, i.e. that the signs he stipulated would determine his actions. We find a similar occurrence with Yonathan son of King Sha-ul in Samuel I 14,10, where he does not rely on the אות which he had stipulated, but where he asked G’d in prayer for help, saying that if certain things were to happen he would regard this as a sign that his prayer had been answered favourably (compare Ibn Ezra there) When the Talmud Chulin 95 כל נחש שאינו כאליעזר עבד אברהם ויהונתן בן שאול אינו נחש, the meaning is that “any divination which is not like that of Eliezer or that of Yonatan ben Sha-ul is not a divination,” i.e. is not permissible, but is akin to relying on witchcraft [Unless the person requesting a sign does so as a prayer directed to G’d it is forbidden. Ed.].


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:2)


**וְגַם גְּמַלֶּיךָ אַשְׁקֶה.** כִּי רָאוּי לַשּׁוֹאֵל שֶׁיִּשְׁאַל פָּחוֹת מִצָּרְכּוֹ שֶׁלֹּא לְהַטְרִיחַ, וְרָאוּי לַנָּדִיב שֶׁיּוֹסִיף וְיַעֲשֶׂה לַשּׁוֹאֵל דֵּי מַחְסוֹרוֹ אוֹ יוֹתֵר.


**I will also water your camels.** A supplicant should ask for less than his needs and the giver should provide him with all his needs or more.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:14:3)


**אוֹתָהּ הוֹכַחְתָּ.** יִסַּרְתָּ וְהוֹרֵיתָ אוֹתָהּ מוּסַר הַשְּׂכֵּל, בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁתִּהְיֶה רְאוּיָה לְעַבְדְּךָ לְיִצְחָק.


**Whom You have determined.** *Hochachta* in Hebrew can also be rendered “whom You have rebuked” — You admonished and instructed her until she was worthy of marrying Yitzchok.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:15:1)


**טֶרֶם כִּלָּה.** עֲדַיִן לֹא כִּלָּה, עַל דֶּרֶךְ "וְהָיָה טֶרֶם יִקְרָאוּ וַאֲנִי אֶעֱנֶה" (ישעיהו סה:כד).


טרם כלה, he had not yet finished. This is what Isaiah 65,24 had in mind when he said that G’d answers a prayer before it has been articulated.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:15:2)


**אֲשֶׁר יֻלְּדָה לִבְתוּאֵל בֶּן מִלְכָּה.** בֶּן הַגְּבֶרֶת, לֹא בֶּן רְאוּמָה הַפִּילֶגֶשׁ.


אשר ילדה לבתואל בן מלכה, he was not a son of Re-umah the concubine. [if the Torah had not wanted to make this point, it could simply have written Betuel, son of Nachor.]



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:16:1)


**טוֹבַת מַרְאֶה.** יְפַת הַצֶּבַע.


טובת מראה, she had beautiful skin colouring.



### Verse 19

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:19:1)


**וַתְּכַל לְהַשְׁקוֹתוֹ וַתֹּאמֶר.** הִמְתִּינָה לְדַבֵּר עַד שֶׁיִּכְלֶה לִשְׁתּוֹת, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה אֵין מְשִׂיחִין בִּסְעוּדָה שֶׁמָּא יַקְדִּים קָנֶה לְוֵשֶׁט (תענית ה).


ותכל להשקותו ותאמר, she waited with speaking until Eliezer had finished drinking his fill. She followed the dictum of our sages that one should not engage in conversation while eating (during the meal) (Taanit 5)



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:20:1)


**וַתְּמַהֵר.** כִּי הַמְּהִירוּת בַּעֲבוֹדַת הַמְשָׁרֵת יוֹרֶה עַל הֱיוֹת הַנֶּעֱבָד נֶחְשָׁב אֶצְלוֹ.


ותמהר, by being quick in what one does, a person performing a service indicates that the one whom she serves is someone esteemed highly in his eyes. Rivkah expressed this by the speed with which she went about her tasks.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:20:2)


**וַתָּרָץ עוֹד אֶל הַבְּאֵר.** אֲשֶׁר לִפְנֵי הַמַּעְיָן שֶׁמִּמֶּנּוּ יִשְׁתּוּ הַבְּהֵמוֹת:


ותרץ עוד אל הבאר, the one in front of the well ((מעיין which was designed for the beasts to drink from.



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:1)


**מִשְׁתָּאֵה.** מִשְׁתּוֹמֵם עַל רוֹב הַזְּרִיזוּת לַעֲשׂוֹת חֶסֶד.


**Wondering at her.** He was amazed at her zeal.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:2)


**מַחֲרִישׁ.** וְלֹא אָמַר לָהּ אַל תִּטְרְחִי כָּל כָּךְ כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה רָאוּי בְּחֹק הַמּוּסָר, לָדַעַת לְהַכִּיר מִתּוֹךְ אוֹפֶן חֲסִידוּתָהּ וּזְרִיזוּתָהּ.


**Remained silent.** He did not try to dissuade her from troubling herself. **Waiting to determine.** The way she performed her good deeds was to be the test.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:3)


**הַהִצְלִיחַ ה' דַּרְכּוֹ.** אִם יִהְיֶה עַל צַד הַטּוֹב וְהַחֶסֶד הַגָּמוּר.


**Whether Ad-noy had made.** If she acted out of pure kindness he would know that his mission was successful.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:21:4)

**Or not.** If she expected payment she was not the right one



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:22:1)


**כַּאֲשֶׁר כִּלּוּ הַגְּמַלִּים לִשְׁתּוֹת.** זֶה הָיָה זְמַן מָה בְּהֶכְרֵחַ אַחַר שֶׁהִשְׁלִימָה לִשְׁאוֹב, וְרָאָה שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה מְבַקֶּשֶׁת דָּבָר, אֲבָל הָיוּ כָּל מַעֲשֶׂיהָ עַל צַד הַחֶסֶד הַגָּמוּר.


**When the camels had finished.** He did not give her the jewelry immediately after she finished drawing the water because he was waiting to see if she would ask for remuneration.


###### Sforno on Genesis 24:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:22:2)


**וּשְׁנֵי צְמִידִים עַל יָדֶיהָ.** רְאוּיִים לְמִדַּת יָדֶיהָ, כְּמוֹ "וְעָשִׂיתָ עַל הַחֹשֶׁן שַׁרְשֹׁת גַּבְלוּת" (שמות כח:כב), "וְעָשִׂיתָ עָלָיו זֵר זָהָב" (שמות כה:יא) – רְאוּיִים לְמִדּוֹתָם.


ושני צמידים על ידיה, according to the size that fitted her wrists; this is similar to Exodus 28,22 ועשית עלי החושן שרשות גבלות, “you shall construct around it plaited chains of beaten gold,” or Exodus 25,11 ועשית עליו זר זהב, “you shall construct around it a golden border.” In each of these instances the measurements of the chains or border had to fit the pricipal artifact.

היש בית אביך מקום, are they prepared to welcome strangers as guests as was customary in that time?

ללין, so that both we and our camels can stay the night there?

גם תבן גם מספוא, not only do we have room for the camels but we also have feed for them.

גם מקום ללון, both for yourself and the men with you.



### Verse 29

וירץ לבן אל האיש, to take a look at the wealthy visitor that had come to town, not to invite him to his own home.

**And heard the words.** That Eliezer had asked for lodgings.

He did not show any expectation of being granted lodgings until Lavan offered

?למה תעמוד בחוץ, why did you only ask about spending the night? Why would you and your men stand around on the outside?

ואנכי פניתי הבית, for you and your men,

ומקום לגמלים, I also cleared space to accommodate the camels.

ברך את אדוני, there is no question that many notable families in our own land would want their daughters to become the wife my master’s son.

אחרי זקנתו, this is why this son was beloved for him from the day he was born.



### Verse 37

###### Sforno on Genesis 24:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/24:37:1)
