**וַיִּחַר אַפּוֹ.** עַל שֶׁהִתְרַעֲמָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵבִיא לָהּ אִישׁ עִבְרִי לְצַחֵק בָּהּ, כִּי אָמְנָם לֹא חָרָה אַפּוֹ עַל יוֹסֵף בָּזֶה, שֶׁהֶאֱמִין יוֹתֵר לְדִבְרֵי יוֹסֵף, אֲבָל נְתָנוֹ בְּבֵית הַסֹּהַר לְהַרְאוֹת שֶׁהֶאֱמִין לָהּ לִכְבוֹדָהּ, וְנִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בְּיוֹסֵף בְּבֵית הַסֹּהַר כְּאָמְרוֹ: "וַיִּפְקֹד שַׂר הַטַּבָּחִים אֶת יוֹסֵף אִתָּם" (בראשית מ:ד).



## Chapter 40


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:1:1)


**חָטְאוּ מַשְׁקֵה מֶלֶךְ מִצְרַיִם וְהָאוֹפֶה.** עַבְדֵי שַׂר הַמַּשְׁקִים וְעַבְדֵי שַׂר הָאוֹפִים.


חטאו משקה מלך מצרים והאופה, the underlings of the chief cup-bearer and the underlings of the Chief of the bakers were guilty of a misdemeanour.



### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:2:1)


**וַיִּקְצֹף פַּרְעֹה עַל שְׁנֵי סָרִיסָיו.** שֶׁלֹּא הִשְׁגִּיחוּ עַל עַבְדֵיהֶם.


ויקצף פרעה על שני סריסיו, because they had not supervised their underlings carefully, as was their task.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:5:1)


**הַמַּשְׁקֶה וְהָאוֹפֶה... אֲשֶׁר אֲסוּרִים.** חָלְמוּ כְּמוֹ מַשְׁקֶה וְאוֹפֶה, לֹא כְּמוֹ שָׂרִים מְצֻוִּים לְעַבְדֵיהֶם, וְזֶה בִּהְיוֹתָם אֲסוּרִים וְלִבָּם חָלָל בְּקִרְבָּם, בִּלְתִּי מְהַרְהֵר בִּשְׂרָרָה.


סריסי פרעה אשר אתו במשמר בית אדוניו, seeing that his master had entrusted their well being to him, as we know from verse 4 “the official in charge appointed Joseph to be with them.” If it had not been for this, it would not have been appropriate for Joseph to enquire into the private affairs of these ministers awaiting their final sentence.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:7:1)


**סְרִיסֵי פַרְעֹה אֲשֶׁר אִתּוֹ בְמִשְׁמַר בֵּית אֲדֹנָיו.** בִּשְׁבִיל שֶׁהִפְקִיד אוֹתָם אֲדוֹנָיו בְּיָדוֹ, כְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיִּפְקֹד שַׂר הַטַּבָּחִים אֶת יוֹסֵף אִתָּם**, כִּי לוּלֵא זֶה לֹא הָיָה רָאוּי לְיוֹסֵף לַחֲקֹר עַל מַחְשְׁבוֹתֵיהֶם:


סריסי פרעה אשר אתו במשמר בית אדוניו, seeing that his master had entrusted their well being to him, as we know from verse 4 “the official in charge appointed Joseph to be with them.” If it had not been for this, it would not have been appropriate for Joseph to enquire into the private affairs of these ministers awaiting their final sentence.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:8:1)


**הֲלֹא לֵאלֹהִים פִּתְרוֹנִים.** הִנֵּה חָכְמַת הַפִּתְרוֹן הִיא בָּאָדָם מִצַּד מַה שֶּׁהוּא בְּצֶלֶם אֱלֹהִים, וְלָזֶה יִתָּכֵן שֶׁתִּהְיֶה גַּם בִּי אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֲנִי עַתָּה עֶבֶד וּבְבֵית הָאֲסוּרִים, וּמִפְּנֵי זֶה אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא צְדַקְתֶּם בְּמַה שֶׁאֲמַרְתֶּם **וּפֹתֵר אֵין אֹתוֹ**:


הלא לאלוקים פתרונים? The wisdom to interpret a dream is something divinely inspired, seeing that man has been created in G’d’s image. It is therefore possible that even I may have been endowed with such wisdom, even though I am a lowly servant and on top of my misfortune I am in jail. It may therefore be possible that you erred when you said that there is no one who can interpret your dreams.

###### Sforno on Genesis 40:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/40:16:1)


**כִּי טוֹב פָּתָר.** וְהָיָה מְקַוֶּה שֶׁיִּפְתּוֹר גַּם בַּעֲדוֹ לְטוֹב, שֶׁהַחֲלוֹמוֹת הוֹלְכִים אַחַר הַפֶּה כְּדִבְרֵיהֶם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה, כְּאָמְרוֹ **כַּאֲשֶׁר פָּתַר לָהֶם יוֹסֵף**:


כי טוב פתר, he was encouraged to think that Joseph would also come up with positive explanation of his own dream. Our sages use the words “as Joseph had interpreted to them,” (verse 22) to state that most dreams turn out to be in accordance with how the interpreter saw them.



## Chapter 41


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:1:1)


**וַיְהִי מִקֵּץ... וּפַרְעֹה חוֹלֵם.** בִּהְיוֹתוֹ חוֹלֵם עִנְיְנֵי רַעְיוֹנָיו, חָלַם גַּם כֵּן שֶׁהָיָה עוֹמֵד עַל שְׂפַת הַיְאוֹר, כְּאָמְרָם רַבּוֹתֵינוּ זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (ברכות נה.): "כְּשֵׁם שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לְבָר בְּלֹא תֶּבֶן, כָּךְ אִי אֶפְשָׁר לַחֲלוֹם בְּלֹא דְּבָרִים בְּטֵלִים".


ויהי מקץ..ופרעה חולם, while he was dreaming about all kinds of matters of no relevance, matters reflecting what he had experienced during the course of the previous day, he also dreamt something totally unrelated to matters he could relate to, i.e. he saw himself standing on the banks of the river. The word חולם in the present tense, as opposed to חלם in the past, recalls Daniel’s statement as well as the Talmud’s statement in Berachot 55 that generally the subjects one dreams about are unfinished business of what one had been thinking of in the course of the previous day.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:3:1)


**וַתַּעֲמֹדְנָה אֵצֶל הַפָּרוֹת.** קוֹדֶם שֶׁתֹּאכַלְנָה אוֹתָנָה, לְהוֹרוֹת שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הָרָעָב וְהַשָּׂבָע יַחְדָּו בְּאֵיזֶה זְמַן, כְּעִנְיַן **וַיְהִי רָעָב בְּכָל הָאֲרָצוֹת וּבְכָל אֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם הָיָה לָחֶם**:


ותעמודנה אצל הפרות, before they had been devoured by the fat cows. This apparently minor nuance was to tell Joseph that famine and plenty would occur at the same time, would overlap at some stage. [the countries surrounding Egypt not having had the benefit of the seven good years, experienced the famine already when there was still plenty of food in Egypt itself. Ed.] (compare verse 54)



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:7:1)


**וְהִנֵּה חֲלוֹם.** הִרְגִּישׁ שֶׁהַכֹּל הָיָה חֲלוֹם אֶחָד בְּעַצְמוֹ, שֶׁהָיָה נִרְאֶה לוֹ בַּחֲלוֹמוֹ הַשֵּׁנִי שֶׁהָיָה בְּאוֹתוֹ הַמַּעֲמָד בְּעַצְמוֹ שֶׁל חֲלוֹמוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְכָךְ פֵּרֵשׁ הוּא אַחַר כָּךְ בְּאָמְרוֹ **וָאֵרֶא בַּחֲלֹמִי**:


והנה חלום, he himself felt that it was all one dream, and that what had been shown him during the second dream was a continuation of the first dream. When he referred to בחלומי, “in my dream,” (sing.) in verse 22 he himself made this point.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:8:1)


**וְאֵין פּוֹתֵר אוֹתָם.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחָשְׁבוּ שֶׁהֵם שְׁנַיִם טָעוּ בַּפִּתְרוֹן, כִּי בַּחֵלֶק הָרִאשׁוֹן מִמֶּנּוּ הָיְתָה הַסִּבָּה הַפּוֹעֶלֶת וְהַחָמְרִית בִּלְבַד, שֶׁהֵן הַפָּרוֹת חוֹרְשׁוֹת וְהַיְאוֹר הַמַּשְׁקֶה, וּבַחֵלֶק הַשֵּׁנִי הָיְתָה הַסִּבָּה הַצּוּרִית וְהַתַּכְלִיתִית בִּלְבַד, וְהֵם הַשִּׁבֳּלִים.


ואין פותר אותם, because they all proceeded from the premise that there had been two dreams. They did not realise that in the first part of the dream the focus was on the active causes of producing food, i.e. ploughing by the cows and threshing of the grain before it could release its kernels, whereas in the second half of the dream the focus was on appearance of the product which is converted into food, the stalks of growing corn, as well as its function, i.e. when the ears are empty there is no food.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:9:1)


**אֶת חֲטָאַי אֲנִי מַזְכִּיר.** לֹא כְּמִתְרָעֵם עַל שֶׁנָּתַתָּ אוֹתִי בְּבֵית הַסֹּהַר, כִּי בְּחֶטְאִי הָיָה.


את חטאי אני מזכיר, not because I want to complain that you, O King, had placed me in prison, for it was my sin that caused this



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:14:1)


**וַיְרִיצֻהוּ מִן הַבּוֹר.** כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל תְּשׁוּעַת ה' שֶׁנַּעֲשֵׂית כְּמוֹ רֶגַע, כְּאָמְרוֹ "כִּי קְרוֹבָה יְשׁוּעָתִי לָבוֹא" (ישעיהו נו:א), וּכְאָמְרוֹ "לוּ עַמִּי שׁוֹמֵעַ לִי וְכוּ' כִּמְעַט אוֹיְבֵיהֶם אַכְנִיעַ" (תהלים פא:יד-טו). וְכָךְ הָיָה עִנְיַן מִצְרַיִם, כְּאָמְרוֹ "כִּי גֹרְשׁוּ מִמִּצְרָיִם" (שמות יב:לט), כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה שֶׁלֹּא הִסְפִּיק בְּצֵקָן שֶׁל אֲבוֹתֵינוּ לְהַחֲמִיץ וְכוּ'. וְכֵן אָמַר לַעֲשׂוֹת לֶעָתִיד, כְּאָמְרוֹ "וּפִתְאוֹם יָבוֹא אֶל הֵיכָלוֹ הָאָדוֹן אֲשֶׁר אַתֶּם מְבַקְשִׁים וְכוּ'" (מלאכי ג:א):


**They hurried him.** A miraculous deliverance always transpires with haste. So it was when the Israelites left Egypt (see *Shemos* 12:39), and so will it be when Yisrael is redeemed from the current exile.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:14:2)


**וַיְחַלֵּף שִׂמְלוֹתָיו.** כִּי אֵין לָבֹא אֶל שַׁעַר הַמֶּלֶךְ בִּלְבוּשׁ שַׂק (אסתר ד:ב).


ויחלף שמלותיו. It is forbidden to appear in the Royal palace clad in sackcloth, prisoner’s garb. [we know this already from Esther chapter 4 when Mordechai, to the consternation of Esther, approached the palace dressed in sackcloth.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:15:1)


**תִּשְׁמַע חֲלוֹם לִפְתּוֹר אוֹתוֹ.** שֶׁתָּבִין עִנְיַן הַחֲלוֹם לִפְתּוֹר אוֹתוֹ כְּפִי חָכְמַת הַפִּתְרוֹן וְלֹא תִּפְתּוֹר בְּמִקְרֶה:


תשמע חלום לפתור אותו, that you understand the principles involved in dreams so that you can use your knowledge to interpret them, not just by a haphazard guess.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:1)


**בִּלְעָדָי.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ **וּפֹתֵר אֵין אֹתוֹ** כְּאִלּוּ אֲנִי יָחִיד בְּזֹאת הַחָכְמָה, אֲנִי חוֹשֵׁב שֶׁיֵּשׁ בְּלִי סָפֵק פּוֹתֵר בִּלְעָדַי.


בלעדי. Even though you have said ופותר אין אותו, that there is no one who knows how to interpret it, as if I were the only exception who possesses the necessary wisdom, I am convinced that there is certainly someone beside me who knows the answer.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:2)


**אֱלֹהִים יַעֲנֶה.** יַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁאֶעֱנֶה בְּפִתְרוֹנִי.


אלוקים יענה, G’d will provide the answer, i.e. whatever I will interpret has been revealed to me by G’d.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:16:3)


**אֶת שְׁלוֹם פַּרְעֹה.** דָּבָר שֶׁיִּהְיֶה שְׁלוֹם פַּרְעֹה, שֶׁהַחֲלוֹמוֹת הוֹלְכִים אַחַר הַפֶּה (ברכות נה:).


את שלום פרעה, I will utter words which will restore Pharaoh’s peace of mind, seeing that the realisation of matters foretold in a dream is determined largely by the words of the interpreter. (Berachot 55)



### Verse 19

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:19:1)


**לֹא רָאִיתִי כָהֵנָּה.** לֹא קָרָה זֶה עַל דֶּרֶךְ רַעְיוֹנָךְ עַל מִשְׁכָּבָךְ סְלִיקוּ (דניאל ב:כט).


לא ראיתי כהנה, Pharaoh meant that such a dream as he had did not reflect anything that he might have thought about during the preceding day, as is usual for most dreams. For what he had seen in his dream was beyond anything he had ever experienced while awake. (compare Daniel 2,29). [the novelty of Seforno’s approach is that he does not understand the word כהנה as referring merely to the frightening appearance of the lean cows. Ed.]



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:21:1)


**וְלֹא נוֹדַע כִּי בָאוּ אֶל קִרְבֶּנָה.** עַל דֶּרֶךְ "וַאֲכַלְתֶּם וְלֹא תִשְׂבָּעוּ" (ויקרא כו:כו), כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה: אֵינוֹ דּוֹמֶה מִי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ פַּת בְּסַלּוֹ לְמִי שֶׁאֵין לוֹ (יומא עד.):


ולא נודע כי באו אל קרבנה, analogous to the warning in Leviticus 26,26 that even when the Israelites would have food, eating it would not satiate them. We also have a statement by our sages describing the effect of food on those who have it (in the fridge) as being different than for those who run out immediately after having eaten their last provisions. The latter cannot feel the effect of what they have eaten because they do not know where their next meal will come from. (Yoma 74)



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:24:1)


**וְאֵין מַגִּיד לִי.** שֶׁרָאָה שֶׁכֻּלָּם חָשְׁבוּ שֶׁהֵם שְׁנֵי חֲלוֹמוֹת וּפָתְרוּ אוֹתָם עַל שְׁנֵי דְבָרִים, וְהוּא הִכִּיר שֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם הָיוּ חֲלוֹם אֶחָד, כְּאָמְרוֹ **וָאֵרֶא בַּחֲלוֹמִי**:


ואין מגיד לי. He noted that all his professional wise men proceeded from the premise that they had to interpret two separate dreams. Pharaoh, however, was convinced that it was one dream as he himself had said when telling it and introducing his narrative in verse 22 with the word בחלומי, “in my dream.” (sing.)



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:25:1)


**חֲלוֹם פַּרְעֹה אֶחָד הוּא.** וּלְפִיכָךְ טָעוּ הַפּוֹתְרִים:


41, 25. חלום פרעה אחד הוא, and this is why all your interpreters have failed to come up with the correct answer.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:25:2)


**אֵת אֲשֶׁר הָאֱלֹהִים עוֹשֶׂה הִגִּיד לְפַרְעֹה.** לְפִיכָךְ לֹא יָדְעוּ זֶה הַחַרְטֻמִּים, כִּי לֹא נוֹדַע לָהֶם בְּלַהֲטֵיהֶם, אֲבָל הִגִּיד לְפַרְעֹה בִּלְבָד.


את אשר אלוקים עושה הגיד לפרעה, the fact that G’d gave Pharaoh advance knowledge of what He was about to do is another reason that your interpreters could not come up with the right interpretation.



### Verse 28

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:28:1)


**הוּא הַדָּבָר.** עִנְיַן הָרָעָב הוּא הַדָּבָר שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי שֶׁהִגִּיד לְפַרְעֹה, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִּכָּרֵת הָאָרֶץ בָּרָעָב:


הוא הדבר, it is the forthcoming famine which I described as the advance information given by G’d to Pharaoh in order to ward off the danger that the famine would destroy Egypt.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:28:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:28:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:28:2)


**הֶרְאָה אֶת פַּרְעֹה.** שְׁנֵי הַשָּׂבָע לְהוֹדִיעוֹ בַּמֶּה יוֹשִׁיעַ אֶת עַמּוֹ מִן הָרָעָב.


הראה את פרעה, the years of plenty, to give you an idea of how to save your people from the devastating effects of the famine.



### Verse 33

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:33:1)


**וְעַתָּה יֵרֶא פַרְעֹה.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁהָאֵל יִתְבָּרַךְ הִגִּיד הָרָעָב הֶעָתִיד לְמַעַן תָּתוּר לְהוֹשִׁיעַ אֶת עַמְּךָ, וְהֶרְאֲךָ הַשָּׂבָע לְהוֹדִיעֲךָ בַּמֶּה תּוֹשִׁיעַ, רָאוּי לְךָ לְמַעַן לֹא תֶּחֱטָא לוֹ שֶׁתִּרְאֶה.


ועתה ירא פרעה, seeing that G’d has seen fit to inform you of the impending famine, no doubt in order to afford you an opportunity to ameliorate its effect on your people, and He has also shown you that this famine will be preceded by seven years of extraordinarily abundant harvests, it is appropriate for you not to become guilty in His eyes if you were to ignore such warning.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:33:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:33:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:33:2)


**אִישׁ נָבוֹן וְחָכָם.** עִיּוּנִי וּמַעֲשִׂי בְּהַנְהָגַת הַמְּדִינוֹת, שֶׁיָּשִׂים הוּא לֵב לָזֶה, וּמַלְכָּא לָא לֶהֱוֵי נָזִיק.


איש נבון וחכם, a wise man, someone possessing the necessary insight of how to put his wisdom to practical use, who will guide the affairs of the country so that the kingdom will not come to harm.



### Verse 34

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:34:1)


**יַעֲשֶׂה פַרְעֹה וְיַפְקֵד.** וְתַעֲשֶׂה שֶׁזֶּה הַנָּבוֹן שֶׁתִּבְחַר יִפְקֹד פְּקִידִים בְּכָל עִיר וָעִיר, לְמַעַן יַכִּירוּהוּ לְשַׂר עֲלֵיהֶם, וְהָיוּ לַאֲחָדִים בְּיָדוֹ בְּכַוָּנָה אַחַת, וְלֹא יִהְיוּ חֲלוּקֵי דֵעוֹת, כְּעִנְיַן "וְקַמְתָּ וְעָלִיתָ אֶל הַמָּקוֹם" (דברים יז:ח).


יעשה פרעה ויפקד, and see to it that this man appoint officials as his delegates in each and every city so that these officials will be known locally as their superiors as well as accepting the authority of their economic czar and that all of them work together in harmony. Special conditions require special measures. Once the Jewish people were dispersed in their own country, the Torah also legislated measures of centralising supreme religious authority in Jerusalem as we know from Deuteronomy 17,8 וקמת ועלית אל המקום אשר יבחר ה' אלוקיך בו, “you will arise and ascend to the place which the Lord your G’d will select for being this the seat of this central authority.”



### Verse 37

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:37:1)


**וַיִּיטַב הַדָּבָר.** עֲצַת יוֹסֵף וּדְרָכָיו.


וייטב הדבר, Joseph’s basic advice as well as his outline of how to execute his plan



### Verse 41

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:41:1)


**רְאֵה נָתַתִּי אֹתְךָ עַל כָּל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.** רְאֵה וְהִתְבּוֹנֵן הֵיטֵב לְהַנְהִיג בְּתַכְלִית הַטּוֹב, כִּי דָבָר גָּדוֹל נָתַתִּי בְּיָדְךָ:


ראה נתתי אותך על כל ארץ מצרים, be aware of the tremendous responsibility I am endowing you with by appointing you as the supreme authority in the whole land of Egypt. [the word ראה, normally translated as “see here,” is used by Pharaoh as telling Joseph to reflect deeply. Ed.]



### Verse 43

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:43:1)


**וַיִּקְרְאוּ לְפָנָיו אַבְרֵךְ.** כְּמוֹ הַבְּרֵךְ, כְּלוֹמַר כָּל אִישׁ יִכְרַע עַל בִּרְכָּיו, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ צוֹעֲקִים לִפְנֵי הַמֶּלֶךְ לוֹמַר לָעָם כִּי לוֹ תִּכְרַע כָּל בֶּרֶךְ.


**Avreich.** This is derived from the word *berech* — “knee” — that is, they ordered the crowds to bend the knee before him.


###### Sforno on Genesis 41:43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:43:2)


**וְנָתוֹן אוֹתוֹ עַל כָּל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.** שֶׁקִּבְּלוּ כֻּלָּם שִׁלְטוֹנוּתוֹ כְּמִנְהָג לְכָל שִׁלְטוֹן חָדָשׁ.


**He thus placed him.** He caused the people to accept Yoseif’s authority.



### Verse 45

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:45:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא יוֹסֵף עַל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.** יָצָא מִלִּפְנֵי פַּרְעֹה בְּאֹפֶן מוֹרֶה שֶׁהָיָה שַׁלִּיט עַל כָּל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.


ויצא יוסף על ארץ מצרים, he walked away from the interview in a manner which indicated that he was now the ruler over the whole nation



### Verse 46

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/41:46:1)


**וַיַּעֲבֹר בְּכָל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.** לִפְקֹד הֶעָרִים וּלְסַדֵּר עִנְיְנֵיהֶם, כְּעִנְיַן "וְסָבַב הַמִּצְפָּה וְעָלָה בֵּית אֵל" (שמואל א ז:טז) בְּעִנְיַן שְׁמוּאֵל.


ויעבר בכל ארץ מצרים. In order to supervise the storage facilities in the cities and to organize matters. We find the prophet Samuel as traveling the length and breadth of the country instead of waiting for the people to come to him in Samuel I 7,16, וסבב בית אל...והמצפה, “and he made the rounds (annually), starting at Bet El and finishing at Mitzpah, his home.”

**לִקְמָצִים.** בְּכָל שִׁבֹּלֶת עָשְׂתָה קְמָצִים.

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:49:1

**אֵין מִסְפָּר.** לֹא הָיָה עוֹד מִסְפָּר נִקְרָא בְּשֵׁם בְּאֹפֶן שֶׁיְּצֻיַּר בַּנֶּפֶשׁ:


אין מספר; the amount was so great that there was no known word to describe such a number, such an amount in terms of grain.

###### Sforno on Genesis 41:51:1

כי נשני אלוקים, what happened to him fore- shadowed what would happen in the future, as described in Isaiah 65,16 כי נשכחו הצרות הראשונות, “for the former troubles shall be forgotten

**וַיִּפְתַּח יוֹסֵף אֶת כָּל אֲשֶׁר בָּהֶם.** לְהַרְאוֹתָם שֶׁהָיָה בְּיָדוֹ כְּדֵי לְפַרְנְסָם.


**Yoseif opened.** He opened all his storehouses to show that he had sufficient grain to feed them all.

**וַיֶּחֱזַק הָרָעָב.** גַּם בִּשְׁאָר מְזוֹנוֹת, בִּלְבַד הַלֶּחֶם.


**The famine became severe.** Foods other than bread also disappeared.



## Chapter 42


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:1:1)


**לָמָּה תִּתְרָאוּ.** לָמָּה תַּבִּיטוּ זֶה אֶל זֶה, וְכָל אֶחָד מִכֶּם מְצַפֶּה שֶׁיֵּלֵךְ חֲבֵירוֹ וְיָתוּר, כְּאָמְרָם זִכְרוֹנָם לִבְרָכָה (עירובין ג) קְדֵרָה דְּשׁוּתְּפֵי לֹא חֲמִימָא וְלֹא קְרִירָא, וְכֵן (מלכים ב יד:ח) "נִתְרָאֶה פָנִים" שֶׁנִּרְאֶה זֶה אֶל זֶה.


וירא...למה תתראו?. Why are you looking at one another as if each one of you hopes that another one would go to buy food? Our sages (Eyruvin 3) have said קדרא דבי ששותפי לא חמימה ולא קררא, an ancient way of saying that “too many cooks spoil the broth.” The conjugation hitpael, i.e. the reflexive conjugation, occurs with the root ראה also in Kings II 14,8 נתראה פנים, “let us confront each other.”



### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:2:1)


**וְנִחְיֶה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיֶה לָשׂבַע, נַשִּׂיג דֵּי מִחְיָתֵנוּ, וּבְאוֹפֶן זֶה **וְלֹא נָמוּת** בָּרָעָב:


ונחיה, even if we cannot buy enough food to eat to our satisfaction, at least it will be enough not to die from the famine. This is why Yaakov added the words ולא נמות, “and we will not die.”



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:3:1)


**וַיֵּרְדוּ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף עֲשָׂרָה.** כִּי לֹא הָיָה הַמַּשְׁבִּיר מוֹכֵר לְשׁוּם קוֹנֶה אֶלָּא בְּעַד בַּיִת אֶחָד וְלֹא בְּעַד רַבִּים, פֶּן יַעֲשֶׂה בּוֹ סְחוֹרָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא הַמִּנְהָג בִּשְׁנֵי רָעָב:


וירדו אחי יוסף עשרה, the official selling grain in Egypt did not sell to more than one family per buyer. The reason for this restriction was intended to ensure that the buyers would not resell some or all of what they had bought, thus using what Joseph had stored as a source of enriching themselves.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:5:1)


**בְּתוֹךְ הַבָּאִים.** שֶׁהָיוּ הוֹלְכִים שָׁם חֲבוּרוֹת יַחַד לְהִמָּלֵט מִן הַשּׁוֹלְלִים שֶׁרַבּוּ אָז מִפְּנֵי הָרָעָב:


בתוך הבאים, people traveled together in large groups to afford themselves protection against highway robbers. At that time there were more than the usual amount of these. They would rob both the money carried to buy food and the food itself



### Verse 6

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:6:1)


**הוּא הַשַּׁלִּיט עַל הָאָרֶץ הוּא הַמַּשְׁבִּיר.** עִם הֱיוֹתוֹ הַשַּׁלִּיט, הָיָה הוּא בְּעַצְמוֹ הַמּוֹכֵר, וְלֹא הָיָה מַאֲמִין בַּעֲבָדָיו, כִּי אָמְנָם הָיָה הַכֶּסֶף רַב מְאֹד וְרָאוּי לְפַרְעֹה, כְּאָמְרוֹ "וַיָּבֵא יוֹסֵף אֶת הַכֶּסֶף בֵּיתָה פַרְעֹה" (בראשית מז:יד), וְלָכֵן לֹא הָיָה שׁוּם בָּר נִמְכָּר לַקּוֹנִים בִּלְתִּי חוֹתָמוֹ אוֹ כְּתַב יָדוֹ.


**He was the one who sold.** He did not trust his servants with the task because the sums involved were too great.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:6:2)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף וַיִּשְׁתַּחֲווּ לוֹ.** וּלְפִי שֶׁעִם הֱיוֹתוֹ שַׁלִּיט הָיָה הוּא בְּעַצְמוֹ הַמַּשְׁבִּיר, הֻצְרְכוּ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף לָבֹא אֵלָיו וְלֹא לְדַבֵּר אֶל עֲבָדָיו בִּלְבָד:


**Yoseif’s brothers came.** Because Yoseif sold the grain personally the brothers were forced to come before him.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:1)


**וַיַּכִּירֵם.** שֶׁהֵם אֶחָיו, לֹא שֶׁהִכִּיר אֶחָד לְאֶחָד:


ויכירם, he recognised them collectively as being his brothers, without being able to recognise who each brother was.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:2)


**וַיִּתְנַכֵּר אֲלֵיהֶם.** שִׁנָּה טַעְמוֹ וְלֹא דִּבֵּר בַּעֲנָוָה כְּמִנְהָגוֹ.


ויתנכר אליהם, he adopted a manner totally uncharacteristic of him, by speaking arrogantly, masking his true voice, etc, apart from the fact that he spoke to them in Egyptian having everything he said translated into Hebrew by an interpreter.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:7:3)


**קָשׁוֹת.** פֶּן יַכִּירוּהוּ בַּקּוֹל.


קשות, so that they would not recognise his voice.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:8:1)


**וַיַּכֵּר יוֹסֵף אֶת אֶחָיו.** אֶחָד לְאֶחָד וְאַחַר כָּךְ:


ויכר יוסף את אחיו, now he recognised them each individually



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:9:1)


**וַיִּזְכֹּר יוֹסֵף אֵת הַחֲלוֹמוֹת אֲשֶׁר חָלַם לָהֶם.** זָכַר לָהֶם אֶת הַחֲלוֹמוֹת, שֶׁבַּחֲלוֹם הָאֲלוּמּוֹת הָיוּ כֻּלָּם מִשְׁתַּחֲוִים, וְשֶׁאֲלֻמָּתוֹ קָמָה וְגַם נִצָּבָה שֶׁלֹּא נָפְלָה אַחַר שֶׁקָּמָה. וְלָכֵן רָצָה שֶׁיָּבֹאוּ כֻּלָּם וִיבַשְּׂרֵם וְיִתְקַיֵּם בּוֹ עִנְיַן "וְגַם נִצָּבָה". וְזֶה בִּזְכוּת שֶׁיִּהְיֶה הוּא רֶמֶז לַגּוֹאֵל הֶעָתִיד עַל דֶּרֶךְ "יְרֵה וַיּוֹר" (מלכים ב יג:יז), כְּאָמְרוֹ "וְנִקְבְּצוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יַחְדָּיו וְשָׂמוּ לָהֶם רֹאשׁ אֶחָד" (הושע ב:ב), וְיִתְקַיֵּם בָּהֶם עִנְיַן "וְגַם נִצָּבָה" כְּאָמְרוֹ "מַלְכוּ דִּי לְעָלְמִין לָא תִתְחַבַּל" (דניאל ב:מד).


ויזכור יוסף את החלמות אשר חלם להם. He remembered the part his brothers had played in his dreams, how they had bowed down to him in the dream showing the sheaves of corn. (37,7) He remembered that only his own sheaf had remained erect, and not fallen after first having arisen. This is why he wanted that they would all come when he would recognise them and the part in the dream speaking of the upright sheaf remaining upright would be fulfilled also. This detail of the dream was due to Joseph symbolising the redeemer of the Jewish people in the future as portrayed by the words ירה ויור, “shoot!, and he shot”. in Kings II 13,17. [this was an opportunity, though missed because of the king of Israel not complying completely with the prophet Elisha’s instructions, of reuniting the kingdoms of Yehudah and the Kingdom of Israel. i.e. that of Ephrayim, the descendant of Joseph. Ed.] There were several such opportunities foreseen by the prophets compare Hoseah 2,2 and Daniel 2,44. The author does not really elaborate. Ed.]

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:9:2)


**לִרְאוֹת אֶת עֶרְוַת הָאָרֶץ בָּאתֶם.** לִרְאוֹת אִם יֵשׁ אִתָּנוּ מָזוֹן מַסְפִּיק לְאַרְצֵנוּ, וְלֹא בָּאתֶם לִקְנוֹת, כִּי אֵין מִנְהַג שְׁאָר הַבָּאִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֲשָׂרָה יַחְדָּיו שׁוֹאֲלִים.


לראות את ערות הארץ באתם, to find out if we have enough food supply for our own country. You did not really come here to buy. It is certainly not the custom of other buyers to arrive in groups of



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:1)


**כֻּלָּנוּ בְּנֵי אִישׁ אֶחָד נָחְנוּ.** וְאִם הָיִינוּ מְרַגְּלִים הָיָה בְּמִצְוַת אֵיזֶה מֶלֶךְ, וְלֹא יִבְחֲרוּ פְּקִידָיו כָּל הַמְרַגְּלִים בְּנֵי אִישׁ אֶחָד, אֲבָל בָּאנוּ יַחְדָּיו מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָנוּ אַחִים.


כלנו בני איש אחד יחנו, if we were spies we would have to be in the employ of some king. No king would select as his spies all members of the same family. The only reason why we arrived together is because we are all members of the same family.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:2)


**כֵּנִים אֲנַחְנוּ.** בְּכָל עֲסָקֵינוּ:


כנים אנחנו, not only are we 100% above board in all our dealings, but


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:11:3)


**לֹא הָיוּ עֲבָדֶיךָ מְרַגְּלִים.** מֵעוֹלָם, וְאֵין רָאוּי לַחֲשֹׁד אוֹתָנוּ בִּמְרַגְּלוּת עַתָּה.


לא היו עבדיך מרגלים, we have never been spies in the past either, and there is no reason at all of suspecting us to be spies now.



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:12:1)


**לֹא.** אֵין זֶה אֱמֶת שֶׁתִּהְיוּ אַחִים.


לא. It is simply not true that you are brothers.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:12:2)


**כִּי עֶרְוַת הָאָרֶץ בָּאתֶם לִרְאוֹת.** אֲבָל נוֹעַצְתֶּם לוֹמַר שֶׁאַתֶּם אַחִים כְּדֵי לָבֹא יַחְדָּיו וְלִרְאוֹת עֶרְוַת הָאָרֶץ.


כי ערות הארץ באתם לראות, you have agreed among your selves to claim to be brothers so that this would provide you with a pretext to act as spies in the land and to find weak spots in our security system.



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:13:1)


**שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר עֲבָדֶיךָ וְכוּ'.** הִנֵּה מַה שֶּׁאָמַרְנוּ יִתְבָּרֵר, כִּי אָמְנָם **אָבִינוּ** עוֹדֶנּוּ חַי בְּאֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן, וְיַגִּיד הוּא וּשְׁכֵנָיו שֶׁהָיִינוּ שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר בָּנָיו, וְחָסֵר אֶחָד, וְ**הַקָּטֹן** נִשְׁאַר לְכַלְכֵּל, וּבְכָל אֵלֶּה הַפְּרָטִים יִתְבָּרֵר הָאֱמֶת אֶצְלְךָ.


שנים עשר עבדיך אחים, בני איש אחד בארץ כנען, what we have said before can easily be proven. After all, our father is still alive in the land of Canaan. Both he and his neighbours will confirm what we have told you, i.e. that we used to be twelve brothers, that one went missing, and that the youngest remained to look after the affairs of our father’s household. You can confirm that we have spoken the truth by checking out all these details.



### Verse 14

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:14:1)


**הוּא אֲשֶׁר דִּבַּרְתִּי אֲלֵיכֶם.** אוֹתוֹ הָאֶחָד שֶׁאַתֶּם אוֹמְרִים שֶׁאֵינֶנּוּ וְאֵינְכֶם מְפָרְשִׁים אָנָה הָלַךְ, הוּא שֶׁהָלַךְ בַּעֲצַתְכֶם לְהַגִּיד מַה שֶּׁרְאִיתֶם, אוֹ שֶׁהִסְכַּמְתֶּם כְּדֵי לְרַגֵּל כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי:


הוא אשר דברתי אליכם, the very one of whom you have said that he is no longer, whereas you refused to be specific about what happened to him, is the one who went back to report all that you have seen here, or that you have decided to stay a while in order to engage in spying.

בזאת תבחנו, if he is not your brother, the youngest one of whom you spoke will not endanger his own life to come with you and to share your fate of the death penalty.

את האלוקים אני ירא. Therefore, I will let you take home enough food for your families

**וְלֹא תָמוּתוּ.** כִּי גַּם בְּאֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן אוּכַל לַהֲמִיתְכֶם אִם לֹא תָבֹאוּ:


ולא תמותו, he meant that they would not die here, as he was able to have them killed also in the land of Canaan if they would not come back of their own free will.



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:21:1)


**בְּהִתְחַנְנוֹ אֵלֵינוּ וְלֹא שָׁמָעְנוּ.** וְהָיִינוּ אַכְזָרִים נֶגֶד אָחִינוּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחֲשַׁבְנוּהוּ לְרוֹדֵף הָיָה לָנוּ לְרַחֵם בְּהִתְחַנְּנוֹ, וּכְנֶגֶד מִדַּת אַכְזָרִיּוּתֵינוּ זֶה הָאִישׁ מִתְאַכְזֵר נֶגְדֵּנוּ.


בהתחננו אלינו ולא שמעבו. This made us guilty of being cruel against our brother, even though we considered him halachically in the category of a רודף, someone threatening us. We should have reacted with pity once he started pleading with us. Now, we are being punished by being treated cruelly ourselves by the ruler of this land.



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:22:1)


**הֲלֹא אָמַרְתִּי אֲלֵיכֶם אַל תֶּחֶטְאוּ בַיֶּלֶד?** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לַהֲמִיתְכֶם כְּשֶׁעָשָׂה מַה שֶּׁעָשָׂה כַּאֲשֶׁר חֲשַׁבְתֶּם, אֲבָל הָיוּ מַעֲשָׂיו בַּאֲשֶׁר הוּא יֶלֶד בִּלְתִּי פּוֹעֵל לְתַכְלִית מְכֻוָּן.


הלא אמרתי אליכם אל תחטאו בילד?. He had never intended to cause your death when he had done what he did, as you had imagined. His actions were due to the fact that he was immature, was childish.


###### Sforno on Genesis 42:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:22:2)


**וְגַם דָּמוֹ הִנֵּה נִדְרָשׁ.** אֵין הַחֵטְא הַנִּדְרָשׁ עַתָּה הָאַכְזָרִיּוּת בִּלְבַד כַּאֲשֶׁר חֲשַׁבְתֶּם, אֲבָל גַּם דָּמוֹ שֶׁחֲטָאתֶם לִשְׁפּוֹךְ דָּם נָקִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה בֶּן מָוֶת כַּאֲשֶׁר חֲשַׁבְתֶּם, וְהִנֵּה בְּלִי סָפֵק מֵת בְּעַבְדוּתוֹ׃


וגם דמו הנה נדרש. The sin we are being punished for now is not only our cruelty, as you think, but also his blood, if your actions were the cause of his death, seeing that he was innocent. There can be little doubt that he died in the performance of his tasks as a slave, something he had not been equipped to cope with.

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:25:1)


**וּלְהָשִׁיב כַּסְפֵּיהֶם.** לֹא יִפּוֹל לָשׁוֹן רַבִּים עַל הַכֶּסֶף, אֲבָל יִפּוֹל עַל הַמַּטְבְּעוֹת:


ולהשיב את כספיהם, and to restore their money. The plural mode in the word does not apply to the collective term כסף, money, but to the coins which together made up the money.



### Verse 28

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:28:1)


**מַה זֹּאת עָשָׂה אֱלֹהִים לָנוּ.** שֶׁנָּתַן בְּלֵב זֶה עִם הֱיוֹתוֹ יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים שֶׁיַּעֲשֶׂה לָנוּ זֹאת כְּדֵי לִכְבּוֹשׁ אוֹתָנוּ לַעֲבָדִים בְּבוֹאֵנוּ אֵלָיו, כְּאָמְרָם אַחַר כָּךְ (בראשית מג:יח) "וְלָקַחַת אוֹתָנוּ לַעֲבָדִים", וְזֶה לְשַׁלֵּם לָנוּ מִדָּה כְּנֶגֶד מִדָּה עַל מְכִירַת אָחִינוּ. וְהִנֵּה הַמְּכִירָה לֹא הָיְתָה מֵאִתָּנוּ לְהָרַע, אֲבָל הָיְתָה מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחֲשַׁבְנוּהוּ לְרוֹדֵף וּבֶן מָוֶת, וּמַה שֶּׁלֹּא הֲרַגְנוּהוּ הָיָה עַל צַד הַחֶסֶד בִּהְיוֹתוֹ אָחִינוּ.


מה זאת עשה אלוקים לנו?. Why would G’d put such ideas in the heart of a person (the ruler) who described himself as “G’d-fearing?” This is also why later, after the goblet had been found in the sack of Binyamin, the brothers offered themselves as slaves to Joseph, feeling that this would be the appropriate punishment for their having sold Joseph into slavery. (43,18). Still, they felt that selling Joseph had not been intended to be detrimental to him; rather seeing they had considered themselves endangered by him as a רודף, they considered this at the time as mild compared to killing him, which they thought they were entitled to do. The very fact that they had not killed him at the time they had considered as proof of their brotherly love



### Verse 36

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:36:1)


**עָלַי הָיוּ כֻלָּנָה.** אֵין אֶחָד מֵאֵלּוּ הַמִּקְרִים בְּאֶחָד מִבְּנֵיכֶם, אֲבָל כֻּלָּם בְּבָנַי. זֶה בְּלִי סָפֵק אֵינוֹ כִּי אִם בְּסִבַּת מְרִיבוֹתֵיכֶם זֶה עִם זֶה, וְאַתֶּם הַמְשַׁכְּלִים אוֹתִי.


עלי היו כלנה, none of these incidents has befallen you, whereas all of them have befallen me. It is therefore clear that the reason these tragedies have all befallen me must be your quarrels with one another. Therefore you are to blame for my being bereaved.



### Verse 37

###### Sforno on Genesis 42:37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/42:37:1)


**אֶת שְׁנֵי בָנַי תָּמִית.** קִלֵּל עַתָּה אֶת שְׁנֵי בָנַי שֶׁיָּמוּתוּ אִם לֹא אֲבִיאֶנּוּ, כְּעִנְיַן קִלְלַת רַב לִשְׁמוּאֵל כְּאָמְרוֹ (שבת קח.) יְהֵא רַעֲוָא דְּלָא לוֹקְמוּ לֵיהּ בְּנֵי, וְכֵן הֲוָה לֵיהּ:


את שני בני תמית, he cursed (conditionally) now two of his sons that they should die if he would not bring back Binyamin safely. The matter is similar to an incident related in Shabbat 108 where Rav said to Shemuel “may it be G’d’s will …that your children will not live.” The curse came true. [The story is extremely enigmatic, seeing that Rav who had thought that Shemuel had treated him not only in a miserly fashion, but had caused him physical discomfort deliberately, had become aware in good time that Shemuel, an outstanding physician, had done what he did in order to relieve an ailment Rav had clearly been suffering from. In spite of this, the conditional curse was fulfilled, much as Yaakov who had cursed the thief who had stolen Lavan’s teraphim not knowing that it had been Rachel. Although the condition had been that if the thief would be found he should not live, Rachel died in spite of the thief (she herself) never having been found. Ed.]

**לֹא יֵרֵד בְּנִי.** הַיָּחִיד לִי מֵאוֹתָהּ הָאֵם שֶׁהָיְתָה עֲקֶרֶת הַבַּיִת:


**My son.** Yaakov refers to Binyamin in this way because he was the only son left from his primary wife [Rochel].



## Chapter 43


### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Genesis 43:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:2:1)


**שׁוּבוּ שִׁבְרוּ לָנוּ.** כִּי חָשַׁב שֶׁהָיוּ מְבַקְּשִׁים לְהוֹלִיךְ עִמָּם אֶת בִּנְיָמִין כְּדֵי לְאַבְּדוֹ כְּיוֹסֵף, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמַר לָהֶם **אֹתִי שִׁכַּלְתֶּם**, לֹא שֶׁהָיָה הַדָּבָר כַּאֲשֶׁר סִפְּרוּ.


שובו שברו לנו, for he thought that their intent in wanting to take Binyamin with them was in order to lose him as they had lost Joseph. He had previously accused them of bereaving him (deliberately) when he had said (42,36) אותי שכלתם, “you have bereaved me,” (transitive active conjugation). He meant to deny that what they had told him at the time had been the truth.

### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Genesis 43:10:1

**כִּי לוּלֵא הִתְמַהְמָהְנוּ.** וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁקִּבַּלְתִּי עָלַי חֵטְא עוֹלָם אִם לֹא הֲבִיאוֹתִיו, הוּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁדָּבָר בָּרוּר אֶצְלִי שֶׁלּוּלֵא הִתְמַהְמַהְנוּ מֵאָז שֶׁסִּפַּרְנוּ אֵלֶיךָ דִּבְרֵי הָאִישׁ אוֹ מֵאָז שֶׁכָּלָה הַשֶּׁבֶר:


כי לולא התמהמנו, the reason I volunteered to consider myself as having sinned against you forever if I fail to bring back Binyamin is only because it is absolutely clear to me that if we had not procrastinated ever since we told you what the man had said to us, or since we ran out of food,


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:10:2)


**כִּי עַתָּה שַׁבְנוּ זֶה פַעֲמָיִם.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה הָאִישׁ מְעַכְּבֵנוּ כְּלָל בִּהְיוֹתוֹ יְרֵא אֱלֹהִים, וְלֹא יוֹסִיף לְהָרַע עִמָּנוּ כַּאֲשֶׁר יֵדַע הָאֱמֶת:


כי עתה שבנו זה פעמים, we could have been back already twice for the man would not have detained us at all, seeing that he described himself as G’d-fearing. He would not continue to harass us once he realised that we had told him the truth.

אם כן, if the matter is indeed as you have said that although the man harassed you he is a G’d-fearing individual,


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:2)


**אֵיפוֹא.** מִן הַהֶכְרֵחַ רָאוּי שֶׁ**זֹּאת** תַּעֲשׂוּ:


איפוא, then it is quite clear what you have to do;


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:3)


**קְחוּ מִזִּמְרַת הָאָרֶץ בִּכְלֵיכֶם מְעַט צֳרִי.** כִּי אָמְנָם הַמִּנְחָה שֶׁתּוּבַל לְאִישׁ נִבְהָל לַהוֹן יִצְטָרֵךְ שֶׁתִּהְיֶה רַבַּת הַכַּמּוּת לְהַשְׂבִּיעַ עַיִן, וּמִזֶּה הַמִּין הָיְתָה מִנְחַת יַעֲקֹב לְעֵשָׂו. אֲבָל כַּאֲשֶׁר יוֹבִילוּהָ לְנָדִיב אֲשֶׁר כֶּסֶף לֹא יַחְשֹׁב, רָאוּי שֶׁתִּהְיֶה מְעַט, אֲבָל תִּהְיֶה דָּבָר נִבְחָר מִדְּבָרִים הַנִּמְצָאִים עַל הַמְעַט וְהֵם בְּהֵיכְלֵי מֶלֶךְ, וּמִזֶּה הַמִּין הָיְתָה הַמִּנְחָה הַזֹּאת לְיוֹסֵף.


קחו מזמרת הארץ מעט צרי, although when one presents a gift to an ordinary person it is important to impress him with the quantity of the gift so that he can feast his eyes on it, this man who has everything will only be impressed with the quality of the gift instead of the quantity; therefore take rare items but only in appropriately small quantities to emphasise their rareness. All the items sent to Joseph were of this nature, as opposed to the gift Yaakov had sent to his brother Esau on a previous occasion.


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:11:4)


**וְהוֹרִידוּ לָאִישׁ מִנְחָה.** קֹדֶם שֶׁתֵּלְכוּ לִרְאוֹת פָּנָיו, לְמַעַן תִּרְאוּ אֵיךְ יְקַבֵּל הַמִּנְחָה בְּרָצוֹן. וּבָזֶה תֵּדְעוּ אֵיךְ יְקַבֵּל אֶתְכֶם בְּסֵבֶר פָּנִים יָפוֹת, כְּאָמְרוֹ (שופטים יג:כג) "לוּ חָפֵץ ה' לַהֲמִיתֵנוּ לֹא לָקַח מִיָּדֵנוּ עוֹלָה וּמִנְחָה". וְאַחַר כָּךְ:


הורידוהו לאיש מנחה, before you will actually meet him face to face again. This will enable you to judge if he receives your gift with goodwill. If he does, you will have reason to be confident that he will receive you also with goodwill. This thought has also been expressed in Judges 13,23 when the wife of Manoach, in trying to calm her husband’s fears, said to him: “if G’d had wanted to kill us He would not have accepted our burnt-offering and our gift-offering first.” After this conversation,

השלום? is he well (healthy) in body? Physical health consists of opposite forces in the body being in perfect balance with one another. [hence the word שלום, “being at peace, being in perfect harmony,” is an acceptable word for describing physical health. Ed.]

אביכם הזקן אשר אמרתם, I am asking, seeing that it is not frequent that aged people are in physically good health. Our sages already stated that “the lips of the aged have a tendency to become worn out and their ears to become hard of hearing.”

### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Genesis 43:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:30:1)


**וַיֵּבְךְּ שָׁמָּה.** שֶׁחָשַׁב בְּצָרַת אָבִיו וְאֶחָיו:


ויבך שמה, while he was thinking of the anguish his father and his brothers had experienced all these years



### Verse 32

###### Sforno on Genesis 43:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:32:1)


**וַיָּשִׂימוּ לוֹ לְבַדּוֹ.** שֶׁלֹּא יַרְגִּישׁוּ אֶחָיו שֶׁהוּא עִבְרִי.


וישימו לו לבדו, in order that his brothers should not notice that he too was a Hebrew.


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:32:2)


**כִּי לֹא יוּכְלוּן הַמִּצְרִים.** לְפִיכָךְ לֹא אָכַל הוּא עִם אֶחָיו, וְלֹא הוּא וְלֹא אֶחָיו עִם הַמִּצְרִים.


כי לא יוכלון המצרים, this is why he did not eat together with his brothers, and neither he nor his brothers ate with the Egyptians.



### Verse 34

###### Sforno on Genesis 43:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:1)


**וַתֵּרֶב מַשְׂאַת בִּנְיָמִין.** לִרְאוֹת אִם יְקַנְאוּ בּוֹ.


ותרב משאת בנימין, to see if the brothers would display signs of jealousy.


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:34:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:2)


**חָמֵשׁ יָדוֹת.** שֶׁעִם כָּל חֵלֶק שֶׁהָיָה שׁוֹלֵחַ לְכָל שְׁנַיִם מֵהֶם כַּמִּנְהָג, הָיָה שׁוֹלֵחַ חֵלֶק לְבִנְיָמִין לְהַרְאוֹת חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ עַל כֻּלָּם:


חמש ידות, five fold; the brothers would be served one serving each for every two of them, whereas Binyamin was given a portion equal in size to the portions served to each set of two brothers. This was Joseph’s way of showing that he considered Binyamin as equal to all of them combined. [the author was at pains to justify why Joseph gave Binyamin five times as much, not 4 times or 6 times, for instance. Ed.]


###### Sforno on Genesis 43:34:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/43:34:3)


**וַיִּשְׁכְּרוּ עִמּוֹ.** בְּמִינֵי יֵין מַלְכוּת רַב שֶׁנָּתַן לִפְנֵיהֶם, שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ מֻרְגָּלִים בָּהֶם, וְלֹא הִשְׁגִּיחוּ שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁתּוֹת כָּל צָרְכָּם מִן הָרִאשׁוֹן, כַּאֲשֶׁר רָאוּי לַעֲשׂוֹת לְכָל סוֹעֵד עִם הַגְּדוֹלִים, כְּאָמְרוֹ: "כִּי תֵשֵׁב לִלְחוֹם אֶת מוֹשֵׁל בִּין תָּבִין אֶת אֲשֶׁר לְפָנֶיךָ" (משלי כג:א).


וישכרו עמו, they were being served the kind of wines drunk by Royalty, something they were not used to, so that it made them drunk. They were careless in not leaving over some of the first kind of wine served them as was the custom for anyone invited to dine with Royalty. Compare Proverbs 23,1 כי תשב ללחום את מושל בין תבין את אשר לפניך, “when you sit down to dine with a ruler, consider well who is before you.”



## Chapter 44


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:1:1)


**וְשִׂים כֶּסֶף אִישׁ בְּפִי אַמְתַּחְתּוֹ.** בִּידִיעָתָם שֶׁתֹּאמַר לָהֶם שֶׁחָפַצְתִּי לְשַׁלֵּם לָהֶם טוֹבָה תְּמוּרַת הַצַּעַר שֶׁצִּעַרְתִּים.


ושים כסף איש בפי אמתחתו, and inform them about it, so that they will realise that I am trying to compensate them for the anguish to which I have subjected them.



### Verse 2

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:2:1)


**תָּשִׂים בְּפִי אַמְתַּחַת הַקָּטֹן.** לִרְאוֹת אֵיךְ יִמְסְרוּ עַצְמָם עָלָיו כְּדֵי לְהַצִּילוֹ:


תשים בפי אמתחת הקטן, in order to test how the brothers would react to Binyamin becoming involved as the victim of a potentially life-threatening accusation, and what the brothers would do in order to save him.



### Verse 5

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:5:1)


**הֲלֹא זֶה אֲשֶׁר יִשְׁתֶּה אֲדֹנִי בּוֹ.** הָיָה דְּבָרוֹ עִמָּהֶם כְּחוֹשֵׁב שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כֻּלָּם יוֹדְעִים הַדָּבָר.


הלא זה אשר ישתה אדוני בו?, He spoke to them as if they all knew that the goblet in question was Joseph’s special goblet.



### Verse 7

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:7:1)


**לָמָּה יְדַבֵּר אֲדֹנִי כַּדְּבָרִים הָאֵלֶּה?** כְּמוֹ חוֹשֵׁד אֶת כֻּלָּנוּ:


למה ידבר אדוני כדברים האלה?, as if he were suspecting all of us?



### Verse 10

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:10:1)


**גַּם עַתָּה כְדִבְרֵיכֶם כֵּן הוּא.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעַתָּה בָּזֶה הָעִנְיָן כֵּן הוּא הַדִּין כְּמוֹ שֶׁאֲמַרְתֶּם, בִּהְיוֹת הַגָּבִיעַ גְּבִיעַ הַשַּׁלִּיט אֲשֶׁר שִׁלֵּם לָכֶם טוֹבָה לְהָשִׁיב כַּסְפְּכֶם אִישׁ אֶל שַׂקּוֹ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם הָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר יִמָּצֵא אִתּוֹ הוּא יִהְיֶה לִּי עֶבֶד וְלֹא כֻּלְּכֶם, וְגַם הוּא לֹא יָמוּת כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיָה מִן הַדִּין.


גם עתה כדבריכם כן הוא. Even though now, in this instance the argument you cite is logical, correct, but in this special circumstance, the goblet in question belonging to the king, a man who has treated you well by giving back all your money in your sacks, the fact is that האיש אש אתו ימצא הגביע הוא יהיה לי עבד, and not all of you. Even this individual will not be sentenced to death as would be required if we applied the law in all its strictness.


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:10:2)


**וְאַתֶּם תִּהְיוּ נְקִיִּים.** מֵעַבְדוּת וּמִכָּל עֹנֶשׁ אַחֵר:


ואתם תהיו נקיים. You will both be free from becoming slaves or any other form of punishment.



### Verse 15

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:15:1)


**מָה הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה.** שֶׁהָיָה בּוֹ רֶשַׁע עִם סִכְלוּת, שֶׁהָיָה לָכֶם לַחְשׁוֹב שֶׁלֹּא יַעֲלֶה זֶה בְּיֶדְכֶם.


**What is this deed.** It was not only wicked but foolish as well, because I am an expert diviner.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:1)


**מַה נֹּאמַר לַאדֹנִי.** לְהָשִׁיב עַל דְּבָרֶיךָ כְּשֶׁאָמַרְתָּ **מָה הַמַּעֲשֶׂה הַזֶּה**, כִּי אָמְנָם?


**What shall we say.** What can we say in response?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:2)


**מַה נְּדַבֵּר.** לְהַכְחִישׁ שֶׁלֹּא עָשִׂינוּ זֹאת אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַדִּין עִמָּנוּ?


מה נדבר? What can we possibly say in justification? How could we prove that we did not do what you accuse us of?


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:3)


**וּמַה נִּצְטַדָּק.** לְהוֹכִיחַ בְּמִישׁוֹר שֶׁהָיָה הַכֹּל תַּחְבּוּלָה לְהִתְעוֹלֵל?


**How can we justify ourselves.** How can we prove that we have been framed?


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:16:4)


**הָאֱלֹהִים מָצָא אֶת עֲוֺן עֲבָדֶיךָ.** לֹא בִּשְׁבִיל זֶה אָנוּ נֶעֱנָשִׁים, שֶׁלֹּא חָטָאנוּ בָּזֶה כְּלָל, אֶלָּא בִּשְׁבִיל עָוֹן שֶׁעָשִׂינוּ לְיָמִים רִאשׁוֹנִים רָצָה הָאֵל לְהַעֲנִישֵׁנוּ לְהִפָּרַע מִמֶּנּוּ עַל יָדְךָ, עַל דֶּרֶךְ "מֵרְשָׁעִים יֵצֵא רֶשַׁע" (שמואל א כד:יד), כְּמַאֲמַר לוּלְיָנוּס וּפַפּוּס (איכה רבתי) בְּאָמְרָם: "אָנוּ הַרְבֵּה נִתְחַיַּבְנוּ מִיתָה לַמָּקוֹם, אֶלָּא הַרְבֵּה שְׁלוּחִים לַמָּקוֹם, נְמֵרִים וַאֲרָיוֹת שֶׁפּוֹגְעִים בָּנוּ, וְלֹא נִפְרָע מִמֶּנּוּ אֶלָּא עַל יָדְךָ שֶׁעָתִיד לִתְבּוֹעַ דָּמֵנוּ מִיָּדְךָ".


**God has found the iniquity.** Hashem is punishing us not for this deed, of which we are innocent, but for a much earlier crime



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:17:1)


**חָלִילָה לִּי מֵעֲשׂוֹת זֹאת.** שֶׁאֶהְיֶה אֲנִי הַשָּׁלִיחַ לְהִפָּרַע מִכֶּם מֵעֲוֹנוֹתֵיכֶם הַקּוֹדְמִים, כְּעִנְיַן "מֵרְשָׁעִים יֵצֵא רֶשַׁע" (שמואל א כד:יד), וְיָדִי לֹא תִהְיֶה בָּךְ. לֹא אֶקַּח לְעֶבֶד וְלֹא אַעֲנִישׁ זוּלָתִי הַחוֹטֵא אֵלַי עַתָּה בָּזֶה הַחֵטְא בִּלְבַד. חסלת פרשת מקץ.


**It would be degrading for me.** I do not wish to be the instrument of chastisement for your earlier crimes because Hashem uses the wicked for that purpose; see *I Shemuel* 24:14.



### Verse 18

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:1)


**וַיִּגַּשׁ אֵלָיו... יְדַבֶּר נָא עַבְדְּךָ דָבָר.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁאָמַרְתָּ **חָלִילָה לִּי מֵעֲשׂוֹת זֹאת**, שֶׁלֹּא תַחְפֹּץ שֶׁתִּהְיֶה תַּקָּלָה עַל יָדְךָ אֲפִלּוּ לְחַיָּבִים.


ויגש אליו. ידבר נא עבדך דבר. After you yourself have said (verse 17) that you do not want to do anything which would pervert justice,


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:2)

ידבר נא עבדך, let me explain to you what unfairness and perversion of justice would result from the course of action you plan.


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:3)


**וְאַל יִחַר אַפְּךָ.** בְּאָמְרִי אֵלֶיךָ שֶׁאַתָּה גָּרַמְתָּ זֹאת הַתַּקָּלָה עַל כָּרְחֵנוּ.


ואל יחר אפך, and do not become angry when I tell you that you will be guilty of this perversion of justice unwittingly.


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:18:4)


**כִּי כָמוֹךָ כְּפַרְעֹה.** כִּי מַה שֶׁאַטִּיחַ דְּבָרִים כְּנֶגְדְּךָ לֹא יִהְיֶה מִהְיוֹתִי בִּלְתִּי מַחְשִׁיב אוֹתְךָ, כִּי אָמְנָם אַתָּה חָשׁוּב בְּעֵינֵי כְּמוֹ פַּרְעֹה שֶׁהוּא הַמֶּלֶךְ.


כי כמוך כפרעה, the fact that I address my words to you is not to be considered personally, as I address them to you in your capacity of representing Pharaoh himself.



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:20:1)


**וְאָבִיו אֲהֵבוֹ.** יוֹתֵר מִכֻּלָּנוּ, וְלָכֵן לֹא הִנִּיחוֹ בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה לָבוֹא עִמָּנוּ, וְזֹאת הָיְתָה הַסִּבָּה שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה עִמָּנוּ אָז, לֹא שֶׁשְּׁלַחְנוּהוּ לְרַגֵּל כַּאֲשֶׁר חָשַׁבְתָּ:


ואביו אהבו, more than he loves all (any) of us combined. Therefore he did not allow him to come to Egypt the first time we came here in order to buy food for our families. The reason was not that we came on a spying mission, as you appear to have thought



### Verse 21

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:21:1)


**וְאָשִׂימָה עֵינִי עָלָיו.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּדְאַג אָבִיו לְשָׁלְחוֹ:


ואשימה עיני עליו. There was therefore no reason why his father should be concerned that he would not be allowed to return home.



### Verse 22

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:22:1)


**לֹא יוּכַל הַנַּעַר לַעֲזֹב אֶת אָבִיו.** כִּי מֵאָז שֶׁיַּעֲזֹב אֶת גַּעֲגוּעֵי אָבִיו וְהַסְבָּרַת פָּנָיו יִתְעַצֵּב וְנָפַל לְמִשְׁכָּב וְאָז יָמוּת:


לא יוכל הנער לעזוב את אביו, from the moment he will have left his father, he will pine for his father and become sick or die. Furthermore,


###### Sforno on Genesis 44:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:22:2)


**וְעָזַב אֶת אָבִיו וָמֵת.** וְעִם זֶה אָבִיו יָמוּת בְּלִי סָפֵק:


ועזב את אביו ומת, also his father will die without question if the lad leaves him.



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:23:1)


**אִם לֹא יֵרֵד.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁשָּׁמַעְתָּ טַעֲנוֹתֵינוּ הָאֲמִתִּיּוֹת, גָּזַרְתָּ עָלֵינוּ בְּרָצוֹן פָּשׁוּט וּבִקְנָס שֶׁנְּבִיאֵהוּ:


אם לא ירד, even though you have heard our true arguments against our bringing him here, you insisted as a form of punishment that we bring him with us on penalty of not getting food.



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:24:1)


**וַנַּגֶּד לוֹ אֵת דִּבְרֵי אֲדֹנִי.** וְאַף עַל פִּי כֵן לֹא שָׁמַע לְשַׁלְּחוֹ אָז.


ונגד לו את דברי אדוני, even after haring that you insisted that we bring him here, our father still refused to let him go with us



### Verse 25

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:25:1)


**שׁוּבוּ שִׁבְרוּ לָנוּ.** וּבְהֶכְרֵחַ הָרָעָב הִכְרַחְנוּהוּ לְשַׁלְּחוֹ, וּבְכָל זֹאת הֵעִיד בָּנוּ שֶׁאוּלַי לֹא נְבִיאֵהוּ אֵלָיו וְנִהְיֶה סִבַּת מִיתָתוֹ בְּיָגוֹן:


שובו שברו לנו; due to the severity of the famine we forced our father to send the lad. In spite of the duress he was under he warned us that if we would not bring him back this would be the death of him and he would go to his grave in deep sorrow



### Verse 30

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:30:1)


**וְעַתָּה כְּבוֹאִי וְהוֹרִידוּ עֲבָדֶיךָ.** מֵאַחַר שֶׁהֵעִיד בָּנוּ הֵנָּה, כַּאֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה כֵּן לֹא יְיוּחַס זֶה אֶל הַמִּקְרֶה, אֲבָל רָאוּי שֶׁיְּיוּחַס לָנוּ שֶׁעָשִׂינוּ זֹאת הַתַּקָּלָה בְּיָדַיִם, כְּעִנְיַן "בְּטֶרֶם תָּבוֹא הִשְׁמַעְתִּיךָ פֶּן תֹּאמַר עָצְבִּי עָשָׂם" (ישעיהו מח:ה).


ועתה כבואי....והורידו עבדיך, after he had warned us, if anything were to happen to him it would no longer be considered an accident but we would be given the blame having been warned not to take him along. A similar consideration is mentioned in Isaiah 48,5 under the heading of בטרם תבא השמעתיך פן תאמר עצבי עשם, “therefore I told you long beforehand, announced things before they happened, that you might not say: “my idol caused them.”



### Verse 32

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:32:1)


**כִּי עַבְדְּךָ עָרַב.** וְהַטַּעַם שֶׁיָּמוּת בִּרְאוֹתוֹ כִּי אֵין הַנַּעַר מִבִּלְתִּי שֶׁיִּשְׁאַל מַה הָיָה לוֹ, הוּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁעַבְדְּךָ עָרֵב, וּמֵאַחַר שֶׁלֹּא אֲבִיאֶנּוּ יַחְשֹׁב שֶׁאָבַד בְּלִי סָפֵק, וְשֶׁבִּשְׁבִיל זֶה לֹא יָכֹלְתִּי לְקַיֵּם נִדְרִי.


כי עבדך ערב, the reason why he would die as soon as the lad did not return without even asking what happened to him is because I have guaranteed the safe return of the lad. As soon as he would notice that that I did not bring him back, he would automatically assume that something fatal had happened to him, and that I had been unable to make good on my vow on account of that.



### Verse 33

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:33:1)


**וְעַתָּה יֵשֶׁב נָא עַבְדְּךָ תַּחַת הַנַּעַר.** לְפִיכָךְ אֲנִי מְבַקֵּשׁ לִהְיוֹת עֶבֶד תַּחְתָּיו, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא אֶחְטָא לְאָבִי כָּל הַיָּמִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁקִּבַּלְתִּי עָלַי:


ועתה ישב נא עבדך תחת הנער, therefore I beg you to let me be the slave in lieu of the lad so that I will not have to remain a sinner against my father for the rest of my life.



### Verse 34

###### Sforno on Genesis 44:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/44:34:1)


**כִּי אֵיךְ אֶעֱלֶה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁחָשַׁבְתִּי שֶׁיִּצְטַעֵר עָלַי, טוֹב לִי שֶׁלֹּא אֶרְאֶנּוּ בְּאוֹתוֹ הַצַּעַר:


כי איך אעלה, even though I know he will be pained by my failing to return it is better for me not to have to watch him in his anguish



## Chapter 45


### Verse 1

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:1:1)


**לְהִתְאַפֵּק לְכֹל הַנִּצָּבִים עָלָיו.** לְהִתְאַפֵּק הִתְאַפְּקוּת מַסְפִּיק לְבָרֵר עִסְקֵי כָּל הַנִּצָּבִים עָלָיו.


להתאפק לכל הנצבים עליו, he did not have the patience at this stage to deal with the private concerns of all the people who were awaiting to receive an audience and were already lining up in that house.



### Verse 3

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:3:1)


**הַעוֹד אָבִי חָי.** אִי אֶפְשָׁר שֶׁלֹּא מֵת מִדַּאֲגָתוֹ עָלַי?


העוד אבי חי?, “how could he have survived so many years of worry over my fate?”



### Verse 4

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:4:1)


**גְּשׁוּ נָא אֵלַי.** שֶׁלֹּא יִשְׁמְעוּ שׁוֹמְעֵי הַבְּכִי אֶת מְכִירָתוֹ:


גשו נא אלי, so that not all the people hearing me cry can see that it is I who is crying.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:4:2)


**אֲשֶׁר מְכַרְתֶּם.** וּבָזֶה תֵּדְעוּ שֶׁאֲנִי יוֹסֵף בְּלִי סָפֵק, שֶׁלֹּא יָדַע אִישׁ בִּמְכִירָתִי וְשֶׁאֲנִי אֲחִיכֶם זוּלָתֵנוּ, כִּי הַקּוֹנִים לֹא יָדְעוּ שֶׁאֲנִי אֲחִיכֶם.


אשר מכרתם, the one whom you have sold! By mentioning this episode you will realise that I can be none other than your brother Joseph, for who else knows about this? The people who bought me as a slave had no idea that I was the sellers’ brother.



### Verse 8

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:1)


**וְעַתָּה לֹא אַתֶּם שְׁלַחְתֶּם.** הִנֵּה בִּרְאוֹתְכֶם תַּכְלִית אֱלֹהִי שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מֻשָּׂג בְּזוּלַת אֵלֶּה הַסִּבּוֹת הַקּוֹדְמוֹת, אֵין סָפֵק שֶׁהַסִּבּוֹת הַקּוֹדְמוֹת הָיוּ גַּם כֵּן בִּרְצוֹן אֱלֹהִי לְסַבֵּב זֶה הַתַּכְלִית.


ועתה לא אתם שלחתם, now that you have seen G’d’s purpose a purpose that could not have been achieved without all the various stages preceding it, it is no more than reasonable to suppose that also the further distant causes which led up to all this were part of G’d’s plan.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:2)


**לְאָב לְפַרְעֹה.** יוֹעֵץ לַמֶּלֶךְ:


לאב לפרעה, as an adviser to the king.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:3)


**וּלְאָדוֹן לְכָל בֵּיתוֹ.** מְמוּנֶּה עַל הַבַּיִת.


ולאדון לכל ביתו, in charge of the entire palace.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:8:4)


**וּמוֹשֵׁל בְּכָל אֶרֶץ מִצְרָיִם.** לְהַנְהִיג עִנְיְנֵי הָעַמִּים.


ומושל בכל ארץ מצרים, and absolute ruler in the whole land of Egypt concerning national and international affairs.



### Verse 9

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:9:1)


**מַהֲרוּ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִצְטַעֵר יוֹתֵר.


מהרו, so that he will not worry and agonise any longer.



### Verse 11

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:11:1)


**פֶּן תִּוָּרֵשׁ.** לְחֶסְרוֹן מִרְעֵה הַצֹּאן, כְּאָמְרָם אַחַר כָּךְ "כִּי אֵין מִרְעֶה לַצֹּאן" (בראשית מז:ד).


פן תורש, on account of the dearth of grazing land in the land of Canaan. (compare 47,4 “there is no grazing land for our flocks.”)



### Verse 12

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:12:1)


**וְעֵינֵי אָחִי בִנְיָמִין.** שֶׁלֹּא יָדַע בִּמְכִירָתִי.


ועיני אחי בנימין, who had been unaware that Joseph had been sold.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:12:2)


**כִּי פִי הַמְדַבֵּר אֲלֵיכֶם.** בִּלְתִּי מֵלִיץ, וְהִנֵּה בִּמְכִירָתִי לֹא הָיָה אִישׁ מְדַבֵּר בִּלְשׁוֹנֵנוּ זוּלָתֵנוּ, כִּי הַקּוֹנִים הָיוּ יִשְׁמְעֵאלִים וּמִדְיָנִים:


כי פי המדבר אליכם, without the need of an interpreter. At the time when I had been sold, no one spoke our language except our family. The buyers were Ishmaelites and Midianites, each with their own language



### Verse 13

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:13:1)

ומהרתם והורדתם את אבי הנה, in order to afford him the joy to see all this with his own eyes.



### Verse 16

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:16:1)


**וַיִּיטַב בְּעֵינֵי פַרְעֹה.** שֶׁחָשַׁב שֶׁמִכָּאן וָאֵילָךְ תִּהְיֶה הַשְׁגָּחַת יוֹסֵף עַל הָאָרֶץ לֹא כְּהַשְׁגָּחַת גֵּר מַנְהִיג, אֲבָל בְּהַשְׁגָּחַת אֶזְרָח. הוּשַׁב לָשֶׁבֶת בָּאָרֶץ הוּא וְזַרְעוֹ, וְלָזֶה יַשְׁגִּיחַ בְּכָל לֵב לְהֵיטִיב לָאָרֶץ וּלְיוֹשְׁבֶיהָ.


**This was good in the eyes of Pharaoh.** He reasoned that if Yoseif’s family settled there he would have more of a stake in the country’s welfare and would perform his duties more diligently.



### Verse 17

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:17:1)


**אֱמוֹר אֶל אַחֶיךָ זֹאת עֲשׂוּ.** שֶׁזֹּאת הִיא הַכַּוָּנָה שֶׁתַּעֲשׂוּ, שֶׁתִּקְחוּ **אֶת אֲבִיכֶם וְאֶת בָּתֵּיכֶם**:


אמור אל אחיך זאת עשו, this is what should be your purpose to take your father and your families, etc.



### Verse 19

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:1)


**וְאַתָּה צֻוֵּיתָה.** וֶאֱמוֹר לָהֶם גַּם כֵּן שֶׁאַתָּה צִוִּיתָ עֲלֵיהֶם:


ואתה צויתה, tell them that you have actually been commanded to bring them here as residents.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:2)


**זֹאת עֲשׂוּ.** וּלְהַשִּׂיג זֶה הַמְכֻוָּן שֶׁלֹּא יְמָאֵן אֲבִיכֶם מִבֹּא (בראשית מה:כז-כח).


קחו לכם מארץ מצרים עגלות, when your father will see carriages all prepared to transport him and his family to Egypt he will have fewer excuses to object to the journey and the upheaval involved in the journey. We know that this was a powerful argument as Yaakov, when seeing the carriages is reported as saying: “I will go down and see my son.” (verse 27-28).


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:19:3)


**קְחוּ לָכֶם מֵאֶרֶץ מִצְרַיִם עֲגָלוֹת.** כִּי כְּשֶׁיִּרְאֶה הָעֲגָלוֹת מוּכָנוֹת שְׁלוּחוֹת מֵאִתָּנוּ, לֹא יִמְצָא כָּל כָּךְ מָקוֹם לִדְחוֹת וּלְמָאֵן. וְכֵן הָיָה אַחַר כָּךְ בְּאָמְרוֹ **וַיַּרְא אֶת הָעֲגָלוֹת אֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח יוֹסֵף לָשֵׂאת אוֹתוֹ וַיֹּאמֶר יִשְׂרָאֵל אֵלְכָה וְאֶרְאֶנּוּ**:



### Verse 20

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:20:1)


**וְעֵינְכֶם אַל תָּחוֹס עַל כְּלֵיכֶם.** שֶׁלֹּא תְּאַחֲרוּ וְיִרְבֶּה הַנֶּזֶק בַּמִּקְנֶה בְּרֹב הָאִחוּר:


ועיניכם אל תחוס על כליכם, do not delay as the resulting damage to your livestock will become greater. the longer you delay.



### Verse 23

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:23:1)


**וּלְאָבִיו שָׁלַח כְּזֹאת.** כְּמוֹ מַתְּנַת בִּנְיָמִין, וְעִם זֶה שָׁלַח עֲשָׂרָה חֲמוֹרִים וְעֶשֶׂר אֲתוֹנוֹת, כִּי בָּרַבִּים תִּהְיֶה הַוָּא"ו בַּסּוֹף כְּמוֹ יִשָּׂשכָר זְבוּלוּן וּבִנְיָמִין (שמות א:ב).


ולאביו שלח כזאת, similar to the gifts given to Binyamin. In addition he sent ten donkeys and ten she-asses. In the plural mode the letter ו indicating who else is included appears at the end [the last word in the sequence. Ed.} Examples of such constructions are: .Exodus 1,2 יששכר זבולון ובנימין.



### Verse 24

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:24:1)


**וַיְשַׁלַּח אֶת אֶחָיו.** פְּטָרָן וְנָתַן לָהֶם רְשׁוּת, כְּמוֹ "שַׁלְּחֵנִי כִּי עָלָה הַשָּׁחַר" (בראשית לב:כז), "שַׁלְּחוּנִי וְאֵלְכָה לַאדֹנִי" (בראשית כד:נו).


וישלח את אחיו, he gave them permission to leave, as when the angel had said to Yaakov (after the wrestling match) שלחני כי עלה השחר, “let me go for dawn has risen.” (32,27) and שלחוני ואלכה לאדוני “let me go, that I may go back to my lord.” (24:56).



### Verse 26

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:26:1)


**וַיָּפָג לִבּוֹ.** נִתְעַלֵּף וְחָסְרָה קְצָת דְּפִיקַת לִבּוֹ וְרוּחוֹ מִמַּה שֶ'הָיָה קוֹדֶם, כַּמִּנְהַג בְּעִלּוּף מִדַּאֲגַת לִבּוֹ בְּהַזְכִּירָם אֶת יוֹסֵף.


ויפג לבות, he passed out. His heart stopped beating briefly, something common when people have a fainting spell. This occurred at the moment Joseph’s name was mentioned by the brothers.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:26:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:26:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:26:2)


**כִּי לֹא הֶאֱמִין לָהֶם.** לְפִיכָךְ **וַתְּחִי רוּחַ יַעֲקֹב**, וְלֹא אָבְדָה רוּחוֹ אַחַר כָּךְ כְּשֶׁהֶאֱמִין, כְּמוֹ שֶׁיִּקְרֶה בְּעֵת הַשִּׂמְחָה הַפִּתְאוֹמִית הַמֵּמִית בְּצֵאת הָרוּחַ הַחִיּוֹנִי אֶל הַחוּץ, כִּי אָמְנָם נִכְנְסָה לִפְנִים בְּעֵת הַדְּאָגָה הַקּוֹדֶמֶת כְּשֶׁלֹּא הֶאֱמִין.


כי לא האמין להם. so that he had no chance to believe them. Therefore, ותחי רוח יעקב, once his spirit revived he never fainted again so that he was able to believe them. It had been the sudden joy that had been the cause of his fainting. Spirit has a habit of contracting when one suffers worry, etc., whereas it expands when one experiences joy, sometimes even beyond the boundaries of one’s body so that the body faints.



### Verse 27

###### Sforno on Genesis 45:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:27:1)


**וַיְדַבְּרוּ אֵלָיו אֵת כָּל דִּבְרֵי יוֹסֵף.** שֶׁאָמַר **וְעוֹד חָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים אֲשֶׁר אֵין חָרִישׁ וְקָצִיר**, כְּדֵי לְעָרֵב בַּדָּבָר הַבְּשׂוֹרָה אֵיזֶהוּ דְאָגָה.


**They told him all the words.** They informed him that there would be another five years of famine in order that he would not faint from excessive joy.


###### Sforno on Genesis 45:27:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:27:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Sforno%20on%20Genesis/r/45:27:2)


**וַתְּחִי רוּחַ יַעֲקֹב.** נִרְפָּא מִן הָעִלּוּף הַקּוֹדֵם בְּהַדְרָגַת הַשִּׂמְחָה עִם הַדְּאָגָה.


ותחי רוח יעקב, he recovered from his fainting spell now that the joy had been tempered by a worrisome element.
